# Sunday Clays Extras Implementation Plan (Plan 11)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or
> superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
> Every implementer and reviewer runs on Opus (master Global Constraints). Each task below is self-contained: it repeats
> every name, command and fixture fact its implementer needs, because an implementer sees only its own task.

**Goal:** Add the weather effects page, next-Sunday predictions, compare / head-to-head / rivals, Year in Review with
"On this day", and share images to the Sunday Clays app, each with its API, UI, Vitest and Playwright coverage.

**Architecture:** Four pure pandas/numpy analytics modules (`weather_effects`, `predictions`, `rivals`, `yir`) sit
behind `@cached_by_data_version` loaders that read the C4 tables with SQL `text()` queries, and four auto-discovered
FastAPI routers expose them at the C8 paths. Five frontend features (`weather`, `predictions`, `compare`, `yir`,
`share`) plug into the app only through the C10 globs (`routes.tsx`, `profileSection.tsx`, `eventSection.tsx`,
`homeWidget.tsx`), draw every data chart inside `ChartFrame`, and keep their controls in the URL. Share images reuse
Plan 07's `lib/share.ts`; Task 5 is the only task that edits another plan's file (Plan 10's `TrophyCard.tsx`) and one
of this plan's own earlier files (`YirCard.tsx`).

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.13 · FastAPI · SQLAlchemy 2 (`text()` queries) · pandas 3 · numpy · pytest + pytest-cov
(testcontainers Postgres 17) · React 19 · TypeScript (strict) · TanStack Query v5 · React Router v7 · openapi-fetch ·
ECharts (tree-shaken `echarts/core`) · Tailwind v4 theme tokens · lucide-react · html-to-image (through
`lib/share.ts`) · Vitest + Testing Library + MSW · Playwright.

**Spec:** `/Users/bryanmoran/code/sunday-clays/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-27-sunday-clays-design.md`

**Master:** `/Users/bryanmoran/code/sunday-clays/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-00-master.md`

## Global Constraints
- Python `3.13`; Node `22` LTS; Postgres `17`; package managers `uv` (backend) and `pnpm` (frontend); lockfiles committed.
- Dependency ownership: Plan 01 T1 (backend) and T2 (frontend) declare the complete Phase-1 dependency set; later tasks never change `[project].dependencies`/`[dependency-groups]` in `backend/pyproject.toml`, `dependencies`/`devDependencies` in `frontend/package.json`, `uv.lock` or `pnpm-lock.yaml` (tool-config sections and scripts may change); a truly new package first lands in a controller `chore(deps): add <pkg>` PR (branch `chore/deps-<pkg>`, manifest + lockfile only) that the task rebases onto; lockfile rebase conflicts are never hand-merged — backend `git checkout origin/main -- backend/uv.lock && (cd backend && uv lock)`, frontend `pnpm install` — then commit (documented in CONTRIBUTING.md, Plan 01 T5); `tools/trophy_art` has its own `pyproject.toml` and is exempt.
- Coverage floor **90% lines AND 90% branches**, backend and frontend measured separately; ratchet may only rise.
- Lint/type: `ruff check` + `ruff format --check` + `mypy --strict` (backend); `eslint` (typescript-eslint strict) + `tsc --noEmit` + `prettier --check` (frontend); warnings held at 0 (`eslint --max-warnings 0`; ruff has no warning level).
- TDD: every production change starts from a failing test (superpowers:test-driven-development).
- Every round is 50 targets; `score` is an int in `0..50`.
- Round type values exactly: `sporting`, `super_sporting`, `unknown`. Rule: any station that day with an odd `target_count` ⇒ `super_sporting`; station data with all even ⇒ `sporting`; no station data ⇒ `unknown`; admin override wins.
- Shooter status values exactly: `member`, `guest`, `deceased`.
- Event dates are SQL `date` (no tz). Weather rows are keyed by local `America/Los_Angeles` wall-clock timestamps. The event window is 10:00–12:00 local and uses the hourly stamps 10:00, 11:00 and 12:00; C7 gives the per-field rules. Stored timestamps are `timestamptz` UTC.
- Units: °F, mph and inches, stored and displayed. Pressure is the one exception: it is stored in hPa (`pressure_hpa`) and displayed in inHg (hPa × 0.02953, 2 dp) via `lib/format.ts:formatPressure` (Plan 07 T4).
- Theme (ClaySmasher defaultBrand): surface `#1A4D2E`, elevated `#2D5F3F`, primary `#9E5530`, primary-container `#C76D3F`, accent `#E8A77A`, text `#FEFBF6`, text-muted `#D4E7DD`, outline `#D1E7DA`, outline-variant `#4A7C59`, error `#FEE2E2`, error-container `#DC2626`; font Roboto; radii card 12px, button 20px, sheet 16px. Dark-only UI.
- Viewports: mobile `390×844` and desktop `1440×900` are both first-class; every page has an e2e check at both.
- Host `sundayclays.claysmasher.com`; compose services exactly `caddy`, `api`, `worker`, `db`, `backup`; configuration only via env vars and `*_FILE` Docker secrets; no host bind mounts in `compose.yaml`. In the Swarm, TLS terminates at the existing Traefik (`traefik_public` external network, labels `traefik.http.routers.sundayclays.rule=Host(\`sundayclays.claysmasher.com\`)`, `entrypoints=websecure`, `tls.certresolver=cloudflare`); Caddy listens on plain `:80` inside the stack.
- Trophy art comes from imagen at `https://imagen.thehalf.io` via the dev-only tool `tools/trophy_art/` (credentials from env `IMAGEN_URL`, `IMAGEN_USERNAME`, `IMAGEN_PASSWORD`, never committed); the running app and CI never call imagen.
- Auth: exactly two roles, `viewer` and `admin` (admin ⊇ viewer); passwords supplied as argon2 hashes via secrets.
- Do NOT add a Claude code-review GitHub workflow.
- Branch per task `task/<NN>-<T>-<slug>` (NN = sub-plan, T = task); controller-only branches `chore/ratchet-wave-<N>` (the only branches that may change `ratchets/`); Conventional Commits; commit trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Agents: every implementer, reviewer, fix-loop escalation, plan author, critic and verifier runs on Opus — never pass a cheaper `model` to Agent or workflow `agent()`, whatever a skill's model-selection guidance says.
- PR topology: each dependency chain is one GitHub stacked PR stack (`gh stack`, extension `github/gh-stack`, installed 2026-09-27 on gh 2.101.0); independent chains are separate stacks off `main`; `ci.yml`'s `pull_request` trigger has no `branches:` filter so mid-stack PRs (whose base is another stack branch) run full CI.
- Migrations must be expand/contract compatible with the previous release's code (`api` runs `alembic upgrade head` on start, before uvicorn).
- Test fixtures are the real workbooks at `backend/tests/fixtures/scores_2026-09-27.xlsx` and `backend/tests/fixtures/stations_2026-09-27.xlsx` (copies of the originals; originals stay at repo root until the user deletes them).
- Wave 3 carries for UI tasks: every data chart renders in `ChartFrame`/`ChartCard` (Plan 07 T6), and each page's chart test uses `expectChartControls` from `src/test/charts.ts`; in-app links use `useRoundTypeLink`/`useRoundTypeHref` (`lib/roundTypes.ts`). Fixture facts: 7,480 rounds, 332 shooters, 360 events (310 held); gauge Sub-Gauge 9, 28 Gauge 8. Known gap, parked at the Wave 3 final review: the fullscreen view does not show ChartCard's chips or the truncation note.

## Review Focus
None of the master's five Review Focus items is owned by Plan 11. These five plan-specific failure modes are the ones
most likely to bite a person using these pages; each is pinned by the named tests in the owning task.
1. **No forecast, no analytics run and no weather yet** (a fresh install, `WEATHER_ENABLED=false` in CI e2e, a forecast
   window with a missing hour) → every page and endpoint answers with an explicit empty state, never a 500 or a blank
   card; predictions fall back from the weather model to the intercept-only fit to the skill prior. Owner: Task 1
   (`test_predictions_before_pipeline_are_empty`, `test_incomplete_forecast_window_is_ignored`,
   `test_forecast_difficulty_falls_back_to_prior`, Vitest `explains that predictions need an analytics run`) and
   Task 2 (`test_weather_endpoints_empty_without_weather`, Vitest `shows the empty state before any Sunday has
   weather`).
2. **A fresh forecast without a data change** (the scheduler refreshes `forecast_cache` twice a day; `data_version`
   does not move) → the Next Sunday card must show the new forecast and the new difficulty, never a memo from the
   earlier fetch. Owner: Task 1 (`test_prediction_cache_key_includes_forecast_fetched_at`).
3. **Degenerate regressions** (a covariate that never varies, fewer events than parameters, one shooter with enough
   rounds, a club SD of zero, a rank-deficient per-shooter design) → no `LinAlgError`, no NaN in JSON; the fit reports
   "no model" or leaves that covariate out, and shrinkage returns 0. Owner: Task 2
   (`test_fit_returns_none_when_covariate_constant`, `test_fit_returns_none_with_too_few_events`,
   `test_sensitivity_drops_constant_covariate`, `test_sensitivity_rank_deficient_fit_has_no_betas`,
   `test_eb_shrink_single_shooter_shrinks_to_zero`, `test_sensitivity_zero_club_sd_leaves_covariate_out`).
4. **Invalid compare input from a hand-edited URL** (one id, the same id twice, five ids, an unknown id, `a == b`) →
   400/404 with a readable message that the page shows, never a 500. Owner: Task 3
   (`test_compare_rejects_bad_id_counts`, `test_h2h_same_shooter_is_400`, `test_unknown_shooter_is_404`, Vitest
   `shows the server message when the comparison fails`).
5. **Ties and multi-round days in head-to-head, "On this day" and personal bests** → a shooter's best round of the day
   is the one compared, tied winners are all listed, and a day with two rounds counts as one PB day. Owner: Task 3
   (`test_head_to_head_uses_best_round_per_day`) and Task 4 (`test_on_this_day_lists_all_tied_winners`,
   `test_pb_days_once_per_day_after_five_rounds`).

## Decisions
Choices made where the master is silent. **(X)** marks a name consumed from another plan; each was checked against
that producer plan's text on 2026-09-27 (cross-plan interface audit), and every task's Step 0 greps the merged code
for it again before any test is written.

1. **(X) Plan 05 names (Task 1):** `sunday_clays.weather.forecast.next_event_sunday(now_local: datetime) -> date`,
   `load_forecast(session, target_date) -> StoredForecast | None` (`StoredForecast.hours: tuple[HourlyObs, ...]`,
   `.fetched_at: datetime`), `refresh_forecast(session, source, *, now_local) -> date` (tests only);
   `sunday_clays.weather.aggregate.aggregate_window(event_date, hours) -> WindowWeather | None` and the frozen
   dataclass `WindowWeather(temp_f, apparent_f, precip_in, wind_mph, gust_mph, wind_dir_deg, cloud_pct, humidity_pct,
   pressure_hpa, condition)`; `sunday_clays.weather.client.HourlyObs` (tests only; `ts_local` is naive local time).
2. **(X) Plan 06 names beyond the contract:** `frames.load_rounds` also returns `held` (the `run_skill_model` input
   column s30 uses) and `gauge`; s30 stores `app_state.skill_params` as `dataclasses.asdict(SkillParams)` and
   `app_state.skill_level` as a JSON number; `skill.run_skill_model(...).final_state` is the raw-scale state
   `skill.predict` takes; `frames.load_rating_history` has `shooter_id, event_date, mu, var`. Frontends read
   `GET /api/shooters` (`shooter_id`, `display_name`), `GET /api/shooters/{id}` (`display_name`, `deceased`,
   `odometer.{clays_broken, rounds, events, years_active, longest_streak}`, `pbs[].{scope, score, event_date}`) and
   `GET /api/events/{date}` (`event_date`, `round_type`, `has_scores`, `head_count`, `n_shooters`, `median`,
   `results[].{round_id, display_name, score, is_best_round, event_rank}`). The generated `paths` keys for those two
   routes are C8's `/api/events/{date}` and `/api/shooters/{id}`: Plan 06 T5/T6/T8 bind them as
   `event_date: Annotated[date, Path(alias="date")]` and `shooter_id: Annotated[int, Path(alias="id")]` (Plan 06
   Decision 22, Plan 08 D3). `PbOut.key` is `"all"` for the overall PB and the year otherwise. Plan 06 routes resolve
   "today" only through `api/routes/_filters.py` (`today_local(tz)`, `resolve_as_of(as_of, tz)`; tests monkeypatch
   `_filters.today_local`), and Task 4 follows that rule.
3. **(X) Plan 07 frontend names:** `api`, `unwrap` (`src/api/client.ts`); `components`, `paths`
   (`src/api/schema.d.ts`, generated by `pnpm gen:api`); `useUrlState(key, codec, default)` with `intCodec`,
   `enumCodec`, `listCodec(item)` (comma list) and `rangeCodec` (`lo..hi`) (`src/lib/useUrlState.ts`);
   `useRoundTypes()` (`src/lib/roundTypes.ts`; C10: every hook for an endpoint that accepts `round_type` sends it and
   puts it in its `queryKey`); `useRoundTypeLink(path: string): To` (same file, Plan 07 T2; C10: every in-app link, to
   events, shooters and so on, takes its target from it rather than a bare path string, so `?rt=` survives navigation;
   a hook, so list rows use a small link component that calls it); `getMe`, `setMe`, `clearMe` (`src/lib/me.ts`);
   `shareElementAsImage(el, filename):
   Promise<ShareOutcome>` (`src/lib/share.ts`; its result is read as `unknown` and only `'cancelled'` is
   special-cased); `NavItem`
   (`src/app/registry.ts`); `ChartFrame` with the C10 props including `actions`; `TabularData`
   (`src/components/charts/types.ts`); `renderWithProviders(ui, { route, path })` (`src/test/render.tsx`); `server`
   (`src/test/msw/server.ts`); e2e `test`/`expect` (`e2e/fixtures.ts`, Plan 01 T4). Plan 01's `vitest.config.ts` sets
   `restoreMocks: true` but not `clearMocks`, and current Vitest's `restoreMocks` never clears a `vi.fn()`'s call
   history, so every test file that reads `mock.calls` resets its module mock in `beforeEach`.
4. **(X) Plan 09:** the Year in Review leader lists call `GET /api/leaderboards?period=season&metric=<m>&as_of=<d>`
   with `metric` in `events`, `avg_score`, `wins`, `season_points`; `as_of` is Dec 31 for a past year and omitted for
   the current year; the global round-type filter is sent as `round_type` (C10), with the same
   `['/api/leaderboards', query]` key shape as Plan 09 T5a's `useLeaderboard`.
5. **(X) Plan 10:** Task 5 wraps `features/achievements/components/TrophyCard.tsx` (props `{ trophy: TrophyOut }`,
   fields `code`, `name`, `label`, `art_key`, `metal`, `holders`, `rarity_pct`) in `ShareCard` and keeps its props and
   its link.
6. **Data access:** analytics loaders and routes read the C4 tables with SQLAlchemy `text()` SQL and never import ORM
   models; every loader is `@cached_by_data_version`; routes resolve dates before calling a memoised function (C7).
7. **Clock seams:** `routes/predictions.py:local_now(tz) -> datetime` is a module function that tests monkeypatch
   (the target needs the time of day, which Plan 06's date-only `_filters` helpers do not give); the prediction target
   is `next_event_sunday(local_now(settings.timezone))`. `GET /api/on-this-day` resolves a missing date with Plan 06's
   `_filters.resolve_as_of(on, settings.timezone)`, and its test monkeypatches `_filters.today_local`.
8. **Response-model names** carry a feature prefix (`Weather*Out`, `Prediction*Out`/`NextPredictionsOut`,
   `Compare*Out`/`H2H*Out`/`Rival*Out`, `Yir*Out`/`OnThisDay*Out`) so OpenAPI schema names never collide across plans.
9. **Predictions:** attendance window = the 13 latest `has_scores` events strictly before the target date, denominator
   always 13, only shooters with p > 0; difficulty source order `weather` (forecast × full club model) → `intercept`
   (intercept-only fit) → `prior` (difficulty `null`, SD √`difficulty_var`) → `none` (no stored params);
   `difficulty_sd` = √σ² of the fit used; `model_ready` = both `skill_params` and `skill_level` stored;
   `skill_snapshot` re-runs `run_skill_model(load_rounds, stored params)` (memoised) to get `final_state`;
   `win_chance = p_win × attend_prob` (NaN odds → 0); rows sorted by win chance, expected, name, id; field median =
   lower weighted median; the memo key of `next_predictions` is (target date, `WindowWeather`, `fetched_at`) plus
   `data_version`; an incomplete forecast window counts as no forecast. The endpoint has no `round_type` parameter.
10. **Club model:** WLS weights = event `n` normalised to mean 1; an event needs `difficulty`, `n > 0` and every
    covariate; `None` when N ≤ p or the design is rank-deficient; SEs from σ²(XᵀWX)⁻¹; the response lists each term's
    mean; precipitation is rounded to 3 dp before banding (float4 storage).
11. **Sensitivity:** rounds need `residual` and all three covariates; z-scores use the club mean and sample SD
    (ddof 1) over every event with weather; a covariate with club SD 0 or no spread in a shooter's rounds is left out
    for that shooter; a rank-deficient design yields no betas; τ² per covariate over the shooters that have a beta, and
    fewer than two such shooters means τ² = 0; `per_unit` = shrunk β × unit / club SD with units 10 °F, 10 mph and
    0.1 in; `cloud_pct` is not a sensitivity covariate (the master's design is [1, z_temp, z_gust, z_precip]).
12. **Bands and turnout:** bands come from `frames.temp_band/wind_band/precip_band` plus the event `condition`; bands
    are ordered by their smallest covariate value, conditions in C7 rule order with unknown labels last; band scores
    use `has_scores` events only; turnout uses every event with weather and a head count (attendance-only days count).
13. **`/api/weather/effects`** de-duplicates and sorts the `round_type` values before the memoised call.
14. **Compare and head-to-head:** a meeting is a date on which both shooters have a best round (C7 best-round rule);
    the result compares those scores (equal = tie); ranks are full-field min-ranks; `avg_margin` = mean(a − b); score
    stats use every filtered round, wins and podiums use best rounds; rating stats use the full, unfiltered history;
    pairs follow `itertools.combinations(ids, 2)`; 2–4 distinct ids else 400 `invalid_compare`, `a == b` → 400
    `invalid_h2h`, unknown id → 404 `shooter_not_found`, missing `ids` → FastAPI's 422.
15. **Rivals:** candidates are shooters with ≥5 meetings (all round types); gap = mean |Δ`mu_before`| over meetings;
    rivalry = meetings / (1 + gap); top 5 by rivalry, meetings, name, id; nemesis = the candidate with the most net
    losses (losses > wins), then meetings, name, id; a candidate without a rating gap can be a nemesis, never a rival.
16. **Year in Review:** calendar years; newcomers = first round in the year and not `left_censored`; top rounds = every
    round with the year's top score; busiest = largest head count (earliest on ties); hardest/easiest = held events by
    published difficulty; trophies = `achievements_awarded` rows dated in the year; `previous` = the prior year when it
    has scored events (club) or rounds (shooter); attendance rank = 1 + shooters with more events that year (min-rank);
    PB days follow C12 `personal_bests` (≥5 earlier rounds, strictly greater, once per day); `rating_start`/`rating_end`
    = last `mu` on or before Dec 31 of the previous / this year; `years` = years with scored events; the path year is
    2000..2100 (422 outside).
17. **On this day:** for 1, 2 and 3 years back, the event (attendance-only too) closest to the same date within ±3 days
    (earlier on ties; Feb 29 → Feb 28); winners = every best round ranked 1, by name; `date` defaults to today in
    `settings.timezone`.
18. **Charts:** feature-local ECharts builders in `features/<name>/charts.ts` return `{ option, data: TabularData }` and
    register what they use with `use as registerECharts` from `echarts/core` (the bare name `use` trips the
    react-hooks lint rule); UI uses plain elements with the Plan 07 theme-token utilities.
19. **MSW ownership:** a feature's `mocks.ts` exports default `handlers` only for this plan's own endpoints; handlers
    for other plans' endpoints are exported under their own names (`directoryHandler` for `/api/shooters`,
    `leaderboardHandler` for `/api/leaderboards`) and installed per test with `server.use`, and `share/fixtures.ts`
    has no handlers at all, so no default handler shadows Plan 06/08/09's.
20. **Glob ids and orders:** profile sections `next-sunday` 20, `rivals` 40, `weather` 50, `share` 95; event section
    `share` 95; home widgets `next-sunday` 10 and `on-this-day` 40 (both slot `main`); routes `/weather` (nav 90),
    `/compare` (100), `/yir`, `/yir/:year`, `/yir/:year/shooters/:id` (110).
21. **URL keys:** conditions explorer `temp` (`lo..hi`), `gust`, `rain`; weather page `wx` (scatter covariate), `wdim`
    (band dimension), `wsc` (sensitivity covariate); compare `ids`; ChartFrame `urlKey`s `next-win`, `wf`, `wr`, `wb`,
    `wt`, `ws`, `cr`, `h2h`, `ym`, `ysm`.
22. **Share filenames:** `sunday-clays-<date>.png` (event), `sunday-clays-<name-slug>.png` (profile),
    `sunday-clays-trophy-<code-slug>.png` (trophy), `sunday-clays-<card-title-slug>.png` (Year in Review cards); the
    button is "Share image", with the card name appended for screen readers when a page has several.
23. **Test layout:** `backend/tests/unit/extras/test_extras_*.py` (pure, no DB) and
    `backend/tests/integration/extras/test_extras_*.py` (`fx_*` world or the empty world); integration tests find
    shooters with `SELECT shooter_id FROM shooter_aliases WHERE name_key = :k`.
24. **Fixture facts** (computed with pandas from the fixture workbooks, not guessed) are listed in the task that asserts
    them.

## Waves
Waves: {T2, T3, T4} → {T1} → {T5}.

Task numbers in this file: Task 2, Task 3 and Task 4 (master T2, T3, T4) run in parallel first; then Task 1 (master
T1, which consumes Task 2's `weather_effects`); then Task 5 (master T5) alone, after Plan 10 T7 and Task 4.
Plan-level dependency: Plans 08, 09 and 10 (except Plan 10 T6) have merged (Global Wave 5).

### Task 1: Next-Sunday predictions — API, home card and profile outlook (master Plan 11 T1)

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/sunday_clays/analytics/predictions.py`
- Create: `backend/src/sunday_clays/api/routes/predictions.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/extras/test_extras_predictions.py`, `backend/tests/integration/extras/test_extras_predictions_route.py`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/predictions/api.ts`, `format.ts`, `charts.ts`, `mocks.ts`, `homeWidget.tsx`, `profileSection.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/predictions/components/NextSundayCard.tsx`, `components/ProfileOutlook.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/src/features/predictions/format.test.ts`, `charts.test.ts`, `glob.test.tsx`, `components/NextSundayCard.test.tsx`, `components/ProfileOutlook.test.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/e2e/predictions.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 11 Task 2, merged before this task): `sunday_clays.analytics.weather_effects`:
  `COVARIATES = ("temp_f", "gust_mph", "precip_in", "cloud_pct")`; `@dataclass(frozen=True) class ClubModel:
  covariates: tuple[str, ...]; coef: tuple[float, ...]; se: tuple[float, ...]; sigma2: float; n_events: int; means:
  tuple[float, ...]` with `predict(x: Mapping[str, float]) -> float` (coef[0] is the intercept);
  `@cached_by_data_version club_regression(session, round_types: tuple[RoundType, ...] = (), covariates:
  tuple[str, ...] = COVARIATES) -> ClubModel | None` (`covariates=()` = the intercept-only fit); route
  `GET /api/weather/effects` → `{model: {terms: [{name, coef, se, mean}], sigma2, n_events} | null, n_events, bands}`.
- Consumes (Plan 05, Decision 1): `sunday_clays.weather.forecast.next_event_sunday(now_local: datetime) -> date`
  (today before 12:00 on a Sunday, else the coming Sunday); `load_forecast(session, target_date: date) ->
  StoredForecast | None` with `StoredForecast(target_date, hours: tuple[HourlyObs, ...], fetched_at: datetime)`;
  `refresh_forecast(session, source: HourlySource, *, now_local: datetime) -> date` (tests only; stores the target
  day's hours with `fetched_at = now_local`); `sunday_clays.weather.aggregate.aggregate_window(event_date, hours) ->
  WindowWeather | None` (None when a 10:00/11:00/12:00 value is missing) and `WindowWeather(temp_f, apparent_f,
  precip_in, wind_mph, gust_mph, wind_dir_deg, cloud_pct, humidity_pct, pressure_hpa, condition)` (frozen);
  `sunday_clays.weather.client.HourlyObs(ts_local, temp_f, apparent_f, precip_in, rain_in, wind_mph, gust_mph,
  wind_dir_deg, cloud_pct, humidity_pct, pressure_hpa, weather_code)` (`ts_local` naive local time) and the protocol
  `HourlySource` (`archive(start, end)`, `forecast(*, past_days, forecast_days)`).
- Consumes (Plan 06 / C7, Decision 2): `sunday_clays.analytics.skill`: `SkillParams(prior_mu, prior_var, obs_var,
  drift_var_per_week, difficulty_var, max_var)`; `run_skill_model(rounds, params) -> SkillResult` (`.final_state:
  dict[int, tuple[float, float, date]]`); `predict(state, shooter_ids, on, params, level, difficulty=None,
  difficulty_sd=0.0, attend_prob=None, n_sims=10_000, seed=0) -> pd.DataFrame` (`shooter_id, attend_prob, expected,
  sd, p_win, p_podium`; odds conditional on attending); `frames.load_rounds(session)` (includes `held`, the
  `run_skill_model` input); `cache.cached_by_data_version`; `app_state` keys `skill_params` (= `asdict(SkillParams)`)
  and `skill_level` (number), both written by s30; `config.get_settings()` (`timezone`); `db.get_session`.
- Carried from Plan 06 (Wave 3): Σ `p_win` over a field exceeds 1 by design, because ties count as wins (C7).
  `skill.predict`'s podium count is O(n²·n_sims), about 0.8 s at 332 shooters: keep the field to shooters with p > 0
  (Decision 9), or replace the count with a sort + `searchsorted` if needed. `SkillParams` validates its variances
  (ValueError); `parse_skill_params` catches it and treats such params as unusable.
- Consumes (Plan 07, Decision 3): `api`, `unwrap` (`src/api/client.ts`); `components` (`src/api/schema.d.ts`);
  `ChartFrame` (`src/components/charts/ChartFrame.tsx`; props `title, subtitle?, option, columns, rows, csvName,
  ariaLabel, urlKey, zoom?, actions?`; renders a region named `title` with `Table` and `CSV` buttons); `TabularData`
  (`src/components/charts/types.ts`); `renderWithProviders(ui, { route?, path? })` (`src/test/render.tsx`); `server`
  (`src/test/msw/server.ts`); e2e `test`/`expect` (`e2e/fixtures.ts`).
- Consumes (Plan 08 T2a/T4 glob hosts): `ProfileSection = { id; title; order; Component: FC<{ shooterId: number }> }`
  (`src/features/shooters/sections.ts`, each section wrapped in a card titled `title`); `HomeWidget = { id; order;
  slot: 'main' | 'me'; Component: FC<{ meId: number | null }> }` (`src/features/home/widgets.ts`).
- Produces: `GET /api/predictions/next` → `NextPredictionsOut { target_date: date; model_ready: bool; forecast:
  PredictionForecastOut | null; difficulty: float | null; difficulty_sd: float | null; difficulty_source: 'weather' |
  'intercept' | 'prior' | 'none'; field_median: float | null; expected_turnout: float; shooters:
  PredictionShooterOut[] }`, `PredictionForecastOut { fetched_at, temp_f, apparent_f, precip_in, wind_mph, gust_mph,
  cloud_pct, condition }`, `PredictionShooterOut { shooter_id, display_name, attend_prob, expected, sd, p_win,
  p_podium, win_chance }` (sorted by `win_chance` desc); `sunday_clays.analytics.predictions`:
  `attendance_probs(rounds, events, on) -> dict[int, float]`, `weighted_median(values, weights) -> float | None`,
  `choose_difficulty(forecast, full, intercept, params) -> DifficultyChoice`, `next_predictions(session,
  target_date, forecast: WindowWeather | None, fetched_at: datetime | None) -> NextPrediction` (memo key includes
  `fetched_at`); frontend `homeWidget` (`id: 'next-sunday'`, `order: 10`, `slot: 'main'`), `profileSection`
  (`id: 'next-sunday'`, `title: 'Next Sunday'`, `order: 20`), `useNextPredictions()`, types `NextPredictions`,
  `PredictedShooter`, `PredictionForecast`, MSW default handler for `*/api/predictions/next`.

(`frames`, `cache`), T3 (`skill.py`), T4 (s30 writes `skill_params`/`skill_level`); Plan 07 T4 (client, test
render), T6 (`ChartFrame`); Plan 08 T2a (profile section glob), T4 (home widget glob); Plan 04 T2 (e2e fixture seed).

**Working rules for this task:**
- Backend integration tests need Docker (testcontainers starts `postgres:17`) or `TEST_DATABASE_URL`. Unit tests never
  touch the DB. `pytest` runs with `filterwarnings = error`.
- `viewer_client` is the empty world. `fx_session`/`fx_viewer_client` are the committed fixture world: both workbooks
  imported, `rebuild_live` and `run_pipeline` done, so `skill_params` and `skill_level` exist; it has no weather rows
  (no network in tests). A test may write inside its savepoint (rolled back at teardown) but must not run the worker.

- Fixture facts (pandas over the fixture workbooks): with "today" pinned to Monday 2026-09-28 09:00
  America/Los_Angeles the target is Sunday 2026-10-04; the 13 latest scored events before it run 2026-06-14 ..

  Kaplan, Noel 11/13, Finnegan, Stanton 4/13, Gilchrist, Melvin none.
- Frontend: Vitest + Testing Library + MSW; every feature's `mocks.ts` `handlers` are merged into the global server;
  a test overrides a response with `server.use(...)`.

```python
"""Plan 11 Task 1: pure next-Sunday prediction rules (no database)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import date, timedelta

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from sunday_clays.analytics.predictions import (
    DifficultyChoice,
    attendance_probs,
    choose_difficulty,
    forecast_covariates,
    parse_skill_params,
    prediction_rows,
    weighted_median,
)
from sunday_clays.analytics.skill import SkillParams
from sunday_clays.analytics.weather_effects import COVARIATES, ClubModel
from sunday_clays.weather.aggregate import WindowWeather

ON = date(2026, 10, 4)
SUNDAYS = [
    ON - timedelta(days=7 * k) for k in range(16, 0, -1)
]  # 16 Sundays before ON, oldest first


def _events(scored: list[date], attendance_only: tuple[date, ...] = ()) -> pd.DataFrame:
    rows = [(d, True) for d in scored] + [(d, False) for d in attendance_only]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["event_date", "has_scores"])


def _rounds(rows: list[tuple[int, date]]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["shooter_id", "event_date"])


def test_attendance_probs_counts_last_13_scored_events() -> None:
    # 16 scored Sundays; the window is the newest 13 (SUNDAYS[3:]); SUNDAYS[10] is attendance-only.
    scored = [d for i, d in enumerate(SUNDAYS) if i != 10]
    events = _events(scored, (SUNDAYS[10],))
    window = scored[-13:]  # SUNDAYS[2], SUNDAYS[3..9], SUNDAYS[11..15]
    rounds = _rounds(
        [(1, d) for d in scored]  # every scored event
        + [(2, SUNDAYS[0]), (2, SUNDAYS[1])]  # only before the window
        + [(3, window[0]), (3, window[-1]), (3, window[-1])]  # 2 dates, 3 rounds
        + [(4, ON)]  # the target day itself does not count
    )
    assert attendance_probs(rounds, events, ON) == {1: 1.0, 3: 2 / 13}


def test_attendance_probs_divides_by_13_even_with_fewer_events() -> None:
    events = _events(SUNDAYS[-3:])
    rounds = _rounds([(7, d) for d in SUNDAYS[-3:]])
    assert attendance_probs(rounds, events, ON) == {7: 3 / 13}


def test_attendance_probs_empty_without_scored_events() -> None:
    assert attendance_probs(_rounds([]), _events([], (SUNDAYS[0],)), ON) == {}


def test_attendance_probs_no_leak() -> None:
    events = _events(SUNDAYS)
    rounds = _rounds([(1, d) for d in SUNDAYS[::2]] + [(2, d) for d in SUNDAYS[-5:]])
    before = attendance_probs(rounds, events, ON)
    later = [ON + timedelta(days=7 * k) for k in range(0, 4)]
    grown_events = pd.concat([events, _events(later)], ignore_index=True)
    grown_rounds = pd.concat([rounds, _rounds([(1, d) for d in later] + [(9, d) for d in later])])
    assert attendance_probs(grown_rounds, grown_events, ON) == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("values", "weights", "expected"),
    [
        pytest.param([30.0, 40.0, 35.0], [1.0, 1.0, 1.0], 35.0, id="odd-equal"),
        pytest.param([30.0, 40.0], [1.0, 1.0], 30.0, id="even-takes-lower"),
        pytest.param([30.0, 40.0], [0.2, 0.9], 40.0, id="weight-moves-median"),
        pytest.param([10.0, 30.0, 40.0], [0.0, 1.0, 1.0], 30.0, id="zero-weight-ignored"),
    ],
)
def test_weighted_median(values: list[float], weights: list[float], expected: float) -> None:
    assert weighted_median(values, weights) == expected


def test_weighted_median_without_weight_is_none() -> None:
    assert weighted_median([], []) is None
    assert weighted_median([31.0], [0.0]) is None


FULL = ClubModel(
    COVARIATES, (1.0, 0.02, 0.1, 3.0, 0.01), (0.1,) * 5, 4.0, 50, (55.0, 12.0, 0.05, 50.0)
)
INTERCEPT = ClubModel((), (0.4,), (0.2,), 2.25, 50, ())
FORECAST = {"temp_f": 50.0, "gust_mph": 20.0, "precip_in": 0.1, "cloud_pct": 80.0}


def test_choose_difficulty_uses_forecast_with_full_model() -> None:
    # 1 + 0.02*50 + 0.1*20 + 3*0.1 + 0.01*80 = 5.1; sd = sqrt(4)
    assert choose_difficulty(FORECAST, FULL, INTERCEPT, SkillParams()) == DifficultyChoice(
        "weather", pytest.approx(5.1), 2.0
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("forecast", "full"),
    [pytest.param(None, FULL, id="no-forecast"), pytest.param(FORECAST, None, id="no-full-model")],
)
def test_choose_difficulty_falls_back_to_intercept(
    forecast: dict[str, float] | None, full: ClubModel | None
) -> None:
    assert choose_difficulty(forecast, full, INTERCEPT, SkillParams()) == DifficultyChoice(
        "intercept", 0.4, 1.5
    )


def test_forecast_difficulty_falls_back_to_prior() -> None:
    params = SkillParams(difficulty_var=9.0)
    assert choose_difficulty(FORECAST, None, None, params) == DifficultyChoice("prior", None, 3.0)
    assert choose_difficulty(None, None, None, None) == DifficultyChoice("none", None, None)


def test_parse_skill_params_round_trips_stored_dict() -> None:
    stored = {
        "prior_mu": 30,
        "prior_var": 49.0,
        "obs_var": 12.0,
        "drift_var_per_week": 0.15,
        "difficulty_var": 2.0,
        "max_var": 49.0,
    }
    assert parse_skill_params(stored) == SkillParams(
        obs_var=12.0, drift_var_per_week=0.15, difficulty_var=2.0
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "stored",
    [
        pytest.param(None, id="absent"),
        pytest.param([30.0], id="not-a-dict"),
        pytest.param({"prior_mu": 30.0}, id="missing-fields"),
        pytest.param(
            {
                **dict.fromkeys(
                    (
                        "prior_mu",
                        "prior_var",
                        "obs_var",
                        "drift_var_per_week",
                        "difficulty_var",
                        "max_var",
                    ),
                    1.0,
                ),
                "obs_var": "x",
            },
            id="not-numeric",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_parse_skill_params_rejects_anything_else(stored: object) -> None:
    assert parse_skill_params(stored) is None


def test_forecast_covariates_reads_the_model_inputs() -> None:
    window = WindowWeather(61.0, 60.0, 0.04, 7.0, 18.0, 200.0, 85.0, 70.0, 1012.0, "rain")
    assert forecast_covariates(window) == {
        "temp_f": 61.0,
        "gust_mph": 18.0,
        "precip_in": 0.04,
        "cloud_pct": 85.0,
    }


def test_prediction_rows_multiply_odds_by_attendance_and_sort() -> None:
    pred = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "shooter_id": [1, 2, 3, 4],
            "attend_prob": [1.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1 / 13],
            "expected": [38.0, 44.0, 38.0, 30.0],
            "sd": [4.0, 4.1, 4.2, 5.0],
            "p_win": [0.2, 0.4, 0.2, math.nan],
            "p_podium": [0.5, 0.7, 0.5, math.nan],
        }
    )
    names = {1: "Baker, Al", 2: "Able, Cy", 3: "Able, Bo", 4: "Zed, Ed"}
    rows = prediction_rows(pred, names)
    # win chances 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.0 -> expected desc (44 first) -> name (Able, Bo < Baker, Al)
    assert [r.shooter_id for r in rows] == [2, 3, 1, 4]
    assert [r.win_chance for r in rows] == [0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.0]
    assert (rows[3].p_win, rows[3].p_podium, rows[3].display_name) == (0.0, 0.0, "Zed, Ed")
    assert (rows[0].attend_prob, rows[0].expected, rows[0].sd) == (0.5, 44.0, 4.1)
```

Create `backend/src/sunday_clays/analytics/predictions.py`:
```python
"""Next-Sunday predictions: attendance, day difficulty from the forecast, and C7 `skill.predict`."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Final, Literal

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from sunday_clays.analytics import frames, skill, weather_effects
from sunday_clays.analytics.cache import cached_by_data_version
from sunday_clays.analytics.skill import SkillParams
from sunday_clays.analytics.weather_effects import ClubModel
from sunday_clays.weather.aggregate import WindowWeather

ATTENDANCE_WINDOW: Final = 13
PARAM_FIELDS: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
    "prior_mu",
    "prior_var",
    "obs_var",
    "drift_var_per_week",
    "difficulty_var",
    "max_var",
)
DifficultySource = Literal["weather", "intercept", "prior", "none"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DifficultyChoice:
    source: DifficultySource
    difficulty: float | None
    sd: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SkillSnapshot:
    params: SkillParams
    level: float
    state: Mapping[int, tuple[float, float, date]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PredictionRow:
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str
    attend_prob: float
    expected: float
    sd: float
    p_win: float
    p_podium: float
    win_chance: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NextPrediction:
    target_date: date
    model_ready: bool
    difficulty: DifficultyChoice
    field_median: float | None
    expected_turnout: float
    shooters: tuple[PredictionRow, ...]


def _as_dates(values: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
    return pd.Series(pd.to_datetime(values).dt.date, index=values.index, dtype=object)


def attendance_probs(rounds: pd.DataFrame, events: pd.DataFrame, on: date) -> dict[int, float]:
    """(# of the last 13 has_scores events before `on` attended) / 13, for shooters with p > 0."""
    event_dates = _as_dates(events["event_date"])
    scored = sorted(
        {d for d, has in zip(event_dates, events["has_scores"], strict=True) if has and d < on}
    )
    window = set(scored[-ATTENDANCE_WINDOW:])
    attended = rounds.assign(event_date=_as_dates(rounds["event_date"]))
    attended = attended[attended["event_date"].isin(window)]
    counts = attended.groupby("shooter_id")["event_date"].nunique()
    ids, attended_counts = counts.index.tolist(), counts.tolist()
    return {int(s): int(n) / ATTENDANCE_WINDOW for s, n in zip(ids, attended_counts, strict=True)}


def weighted_median(values: Sequence[float], weights: Sequence[float]) -> float | None:
    """Lower weighted median: the smallest value whose cumulative weight reaches half the total."""
    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    keep = w > 0
    if not bool(keep.any()):
        return None
    order = np.argsort(v[keep], kind="stable")
    ordered, cumulative = v[keep][order], np.cumsum(w[keep][order])
    return float(ordered[int(np.searchsorted(cumulative, cumulative[-1] / 2.0, side="left"))])


def choose_difficulty(
    forecast: Mapping[str, float] | None,
    full: ClubModel | None,
    intercept: ClubModel | None,
    params: SkillParams | None,
) -> DifficultyChoice:
    """Forecast x club model, else the intercept-only fit, else the skill prior's day variance."""
    if forecast is not None and full is not None:
        return DifficultyChoice("weather", full.predict(forecast), math.sqrt(full.sigma2))
    if intercept is not None:
        return DifficultyChoice("intercept", intercept.coef[0], math.sqrt(intercept.sigma2))
    if params is not None:
        return DifficultyChoice("prior", None, math.sqrt(params.difficulty_var))
    return DifficultyChoice("none", None, None)


def parse_skill_params(value: object) -> SkillParams | None:
    """`app_state.skill_params` (an asdict of SkillParams) back into SkillParams, else None."""
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        return None
    try:
        return SkillParams(**{name: float(value[name]) for name in PARAM_FIELDS})
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def forecast_covariates(window: WindowWeather) -> dict[str, float]:
    return {name: float(getattr(window, name)) for name in weather_effects.COVARIATES}


def _zero_if_nan(value: float) -> float:
    return 0.0 if math.isnan(value) else value


def prediction_rows(pred: pd.DataFrame, names: Mapping[int, str]) -> list[PredictionRow]:
    """Rows from `skill.predict` output; `win_chance = p_win * attend_prob` (NaN odds -> 0)."""
    rows: list[PredictionRow] = []
    for rec in pred.to_dict("records"):
        shooter_id = int(rec["shooter_id"])
        attend = float(rec["attend_prob"])
        p_win = _zero_if_nan(float(rec["p_win"]))
        rows.append(
            PredictionRow(
                shooter_id=shooter_id,
                display_name=names[shooter_id],
                attend_prob=attend,
                expected=float(rec["expected"]),
                sd=float(rec["sd"]),
                p_win=p_win,
                p_podium=_zero_if_nan(float(rec["p_podium"])),
                win_chance=p_win * attend,
            )
        )
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (-r.win_chance, -r.expected, r.display_name, r.shooter_id))
    return rows


def _app_state(session: Session, key: str) -> object:
    return session.execute(
        text("SELECT value FROM app_state WHERE key = :key"), {"key": key}
    ).scalar_one_or_none()


@cached_by_data_version
def skill_snapshot(session: Session) -> SkillSnapshot | None:
    """Re-run the s30 skill model with the stored params; None until s30 has stored both keys."""
    params = parse_skill_params(_app_state(session, "skill_params"))
    level = _app_state(session, "skill_level")
    if params is None or not isinstance(level, (int, float)):
        return None
    result = skill.run_skill_model(frames.load_rounds(session), params)
    return SkillSnapshot(params=params, level=float(level), state=dict(result.final_state))


def _scored_events(session: Session) -> pd.DataFrame:
    rows = session.execute(text("SELECT event_date, has_scores FROM events")).all()
    return pd.DataFrame([tuple(r) for r in rows], columns=["event_date", "has_scores"])


def _display_names(session: Session) -> dict[int, str]:
    rows = session.execute(text("SELECT shooter_id, display_name FROM shooter_profiles")).all()
    return {int(r[0]): str(r[1]) for r in rows}


@cached_by_data_version
def next_predictions(
    session: Session,
    target_date: date,
    forecast: WindowWeather | None,
    fetched_at: datetime | None,
) -> NextPrediction:
    """Predictions for `target_date`; the memo key includes the forecast and `fetched_at` (C7)."""
    snapshot = skill_snapshot(session)
    choice = choose_difficulty(
        None if forecast is None else forecast_covariates(forecast),
        weather_effects.club_regression(session, (), weather_effects.COVARIATES),
        weather_effects.club_regression(session, (), ()),
        None if snapshot is None else snapshot.params,
    )
    if snapshot is None:
        return NextPrediction(target_date, False, choice, None, 0.0, ())
    probs = attendance_probs(frames.load_rounds(session), _scored_events(session), target_date)
    if not probs:
        return NextPrediction(target_date, True, choice, None, 0.0, ())
    pred = skill.predict(
        snapshot.state,
        sorted(probs),
        target_date,
        snapshot.params,
        snapshot.level,
        difficulty=choice.difficulty,
        difficulty_sd=choice.sd or 0.0,
        attend_prob=probs,
    )
    rows = prediction_rows(pred, _display_names(session))
    median = weighted_median([r.expected for r in rows], [r.attend_prob for r in rows])
    return NextPrediction(
        target_date=target_date,
        model_ready=True,
        difficulty=choice,
        field_median=median,
        expected_turnout=float(sum(probs.values())),
        shooters=tuple(rows),
    )
```

Create `backend/tests/integration/extras/test_extras_predictions_route.py` (the forecast hours give a window of
50.0 °F, gust 18.0 mph, 0.02 in and 80 % cloud, so the condition is `rain`):
```python
"""Plan 11 Task 1: GET /api/predictions/next over the empty and committed-fixture worlds."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import date, datetime, time, timedelta
from typing import Any
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from sunday_clays.api.routes import predictions as predictions_route
from sunday_clays.weather.client import HourlyObs
from sunday_clays.weather.forecast import refresh_forecast

LA = ZoneInfo("America/Los_Angeles")
MONDAY = datetime(2026, 9, 28, 9, 0, tzinfo=LA)  # the fixture's last event was 2026-09-27
TARGET = date(2026, 10, 4)
INSERT_2025_ON = text(
    "INSERT INTO event_weather (event_date, temp_f, apparent_f, precip_in, wind_mph, gust_mph,"
    " wind_dir_deg, cloud_pct, humidity_pct, pressure_hpa, condition, source)"
    " SELECT event_date, 30 + EXTRACT(DAY FROM event_date), 28 + EXTRACT(DAY FROM event_date),"
    " CASE WHEN EXTRACT(DAY FROM event_date)::int % 2 = 1 THEN 0.1 ELSE 0 END,"
    " EXTRACT(MONTH FROM event_date), 2 * EXTRACT(MONTH FROM event_date), 200,"
    " (7 * EXTRACT(DAY FROM event_date)::int) % 100, 60, 1015, 'clear', 'archive'"
    " FROM events WHERE results_complete AND event_date >= DATE '2025-01-01'"
)


class FakeForecast:
    """A Plan 05 `HourlySource` returning fixed forecast hours for TARGET."""

    def __init__(self, hours: list[HourlyObs]) -> None:
        self.hours = hours

    def archive(self, start: date, end: date) -> list[HourlyObs]:
        raise AssertionError("predictions never read the archive")

    def forecast(self, *, past_days: int, forecast_days: int) -> list[HourlyObs]:
        return self.hours


def _hours(*, temp: float, gust: float, skip: tuple[int, ...] = ()) -> list[HourlyObs]:
    # Window per C7: mean temp/cloud of 10-12, max gust of 11-12, rain = sum of 11 and 12.
    return [
        HourlyObs(
            ts_local=datetime.combine(TARGET, time(h)),  # naive local wall-clock (Plan 05)
            temp_f=temp,
            apparent_f=temp - 2,
            precip_in=0.01,
            rain_in=0.01,
            wind_mph=gust / 2,
            gust_mph=gust,
            wind_dir_deg=200.0,
            cloud_pct=80.0,
            humidity_pct=90.0,
            pressure_hpa=1010.0,
            weather_code=61,
        )
        for h in range(24)
        if h not in skip
    ]


@pytest.fixture
def monday(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(predictions_route, "local_now", lambda tz: MONDAY)


def _shooter_id(session: Session, name_key: str) -> int:
    return int(
        session.execute(
            text("SELECT shooter_id FROM shooter_aliases WHERE name_key = :k"), {"k": name_key}
        ).scalar_one()
    )


def _get(client: TestClient) -> dict[str, Any]:
    response = client.get("/api/predictions/next")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    body: dict[str, Any] = response.json()
    return body


def test_predictions_before_pipeline_are_empty(viewer_client: TestClient, monday: None) -> None:
    assert _get(viewer_client) == {
        "target_date": "2026-10-04",
        "model_ready": False,
        "forecast": None,
        "difficulty": None,
        "difficulty_sd": None,
        "difficulty_source": "none",
        "field_median": None,
        "expected_turnout": 0.0,
        "shooters": [],
    }


def test_predictions_for_next_sunday_from_the_fixture(
    fx_session: Session, fx_viewer_client: TestClient, monday: None
) -> None:
    body = _get(fx_viewer_client)
    assert (body["target_date"], body["model_ready"], body["forecast"]) == (
        "2026-10-04",
        True,
        None,
    )
    params = fx_session.execute(
        text("SELECT value FROM app_state WHERE key = 'skill_params'")
    ).scalar_one()
    assert (body["difficulty_source"], body["difficulty"]) == ("prior", None)
    assert body["difficulty_sd"] == pytest.approx(math.sqrt(params["difficulty_var"]))
    shooters = body["shooters"]
    # The last 13 scored events before 2026-10-04 run 2026-06-14 .. 2026-09-27; 80 shooters
    # attended at least one of them, 353 attendances in all.
    assert len(shooters) == 80
    assert body["expected_turnout"] == pytest.approx(353 / 13)
    by_id = {s["shooter_id"]: s for s in shooters}
    assert by_id[_shooter_id(fx_session, "hadley ike")]["attend_prob"] == 1.0
    assert by_id[_shooter_id(fx_session, "kaplan noel")]["attend_prob"] == pytest.approx(11 / 13)
    assert by_id[_shooter_id(fx_session, "finnegan stanton")]["attend_prob"] == pytest.approx(4 / 13)
    assert _shooter_id(fx_session, "gilchrist melvin") not in by_id
    for s in shooters:
        assert s["win_chance"] == pytest.approx(s["p_win"] * s["attend_prob"])
        assert 0.0 <= s["p_win"] <= s["p_podium"] <= 1.0
    chances = [s["win_chance"] for s in shooters]
    assert chances == sorted(chances, reverse=True)
    assert body["field_median"] in [s["expected"] for s in shooters]


def test_forecast_sets_weather_difficulty(
    fx_session: Session, fx_viewer_client: TestClient, monday: None
) -> None:
    fx_session.execute(text("DELETE FROM event_weather"))
    fx_session.execute(INSERT_2025_ON)
    refresh_forecast(fx_session, FakeForecast(_hours(temp=50.0, gust=18.0)), now_local=MONDAY)
    body = _get(fx_viewer_client)
    assert body["difficulty_source"] == "weather"
    forecast = body["forecast"]
    assert (forecast["temp_f"], forecast["gust_mph"], forecast["cloud_pct"]) == (50.0, 18.0, 80.0)
    assert (forecast["precip_in"], forecast["condition"]) == (0.02, "rain")
    model = fx_viewer_client.get("/api/weather/effects").json()["model"]
    x = {"intercept": 1.0, "temp_f": 50.0, "gust_mph": 18.0, "precip_in": 0.02, "cloud_pct": 80.0}
    assert body["difficulty"] == pytest.approx(
        sum(t["coef"] * x[t["name"]] for t in model["terms"])
    )
    assert body["difficulty_sd"] == pytest.approx(math.sqrt(model["sigma2"]))


def test_prediction_cache_key_includes_forecast_fetched_at(
    fx_session: Session, fx_viewer_client: TestClient, monday: None
) -> None:
    fx_session.execute(text("DELETE FROM event_weather"))
    fx_session.execute(INSERT_2025_ON)
    version = text("SELECT value FROM app_state WHERE key = 'data_version'")
    before = fx_session.execute(version).scalar_one()
    assert _get(fx_viewer_client)["difficulty_source"] == "intercept"  # memoised: no forecast

    refresh_forecast(fx_session, FakeForecast(_hours(temp=50.0, gust=18.0)), now_local=MONDAY)
    first = _get(fx_viewer_client)
    later = MONDAY + timedelta(hours=6)
    refresh_forecast(fx_session, FakeForecast(_hours(temp=62.0, gust=5.0)), now_local=later)
    second = _get(fx_viewer_client)

    assert fx_session.execute(version).scalar_one() == before  # no rebuild or recompute
    assert first["difficulty_source"] == second["difficulty_source"] == "weather"
    assert first["forecast"]["fetched_at"] != second["forecast"]["fetched_at"]
    assert second["forecast"]["temp_f"] == 62.0
    assert first["difficulty"] != second["difficulty"]


def test_incomplete_forecast_window_is_ignored(
    fx_session: Session, fx_viewer_client: TestClient, monday: None
) -> None:
    fx_session.execute(text("DELETE FROM event_weather"))
    refresh_forecast(
        fx_session, FakeForecast(_hours(temp=50.0, gust=18.0, skip=(11,))), now_local=MONDAY
    )
    body = _get(fx_viewer_client)
    assert body["forecast"] is None
    assert body["difficulty_source"] == "prior"
    assert len(body["shooters"]) == 80


def test_local_now_reads_the_clock_in_the_club_zone() -> None:
    # Every route test pins local_now; this one runs the real clock seam.
    before = datetime.now(LA)
    now = predictions_route.local_now("America/Los_Angeles")
    after = datetime.now(LA)
    assert str(now.tzinfo) == "America/Los_Angeles"
    assert before <= now <= after
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the route**

Create `backend/src/sunday_clays/api/routes/predictions.py` (routers are auto-discovered; `/api/predictions/next` is
already excluded from ETags by Plan 06 T1's middleware per C8):
```python
"""GET /api/predictions/next: next Sunday's forecast, predicted field and win odds."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Annotated, Literal
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from sunday_clays.analytics import predictions
from sunday_clays.config import Settings, get_settings
from sunday_clays.db import get_session
from sunday_clays.weather.aggregate import aggregate_window
from sunday_clays.weather.forecast import load_forecast, next_event_sunday

router = APIRouter()
SessionDep = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_session, scope="function")]
SettingsDep = Annotated[Settings, Depends(get_settings)]


class PredictionForecastOut(BaseModel):
    fetched_at: datetime
    temp_f: float
    apparent_f: float
    precip_in: float
    wind_mph: float
    gust_mph: float
    cloud_pct: float
    condition: str


class PredictionShooterOut(BaseModel):
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str
    attend_prob: float
    expected: float
    sd: float
    p_win: float
    p_podium: float
    win_chance: float


class NextPredictionsOut(BaseModel):
    target_date: date
    model_ready: bool
    forecast: PredictionForecastOut | None
    difficulty: float | None
    difficulty_sd: float | None
    difficulty_source: Literal["weather", "intercept", "prior", "none"]
    field_median: float | None
    expected_turnout: float
    shooters: list[PredictionShooterOut]


def local_now(tz: str) -> datetime:
    """The current wall-clock time in the club's timezone (tests pin this)."""
    return datetime.now(ZoneInfo(tz))


@router.get("/api/predictions/next")
def next_sunday(session: SessionDep, settings: SettingsDep) -> NextPredictionsOut:
    target = next_event_sunday(local_now(settings.timezone))
    stored = load_forecast(session, target)
    window = None if stored is None else aggregate_window(target, stored.hours)
    result = predictions.next_predictions(
        session, target, window, None if stored is None else stored.fetched_at
    )
    forecast = None
    if stored is not None and window is not None:
        forecast = PredictionForecastOut(
            fetched_at=stored.fetched_at,
            temp_f=window.temp_f,
            apparent_f=window.apparent_f,
            precip_in=window.precip_in,
            wind_mph=window.wind_mph,
            gust_mph=window.gust_mph,
            cloud_pct=window.cloud_pct,
            condition=window.condition,
        )
    return NextPredictionsOut(
        target_date=result.target_date,
        model_ready=result.model_ready,
        forecast=forecast,
        difficulty=result.difficulty.difficulty,
        difficulty_sd=result.difficulty.sd,
        difficulty_source=result.difficulty.source,
        field_median=result.field_median,
        expected_turnout=result.expected_turnout,
        shooters=[PredictionShooterOut.model_validate(asdict(r)) for r in result.shooters],
    )
```

```ts
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { expect, test } from './fixtures';

// Runs in the `mobile` and `desktop` projects as the seeded viewer. compose.test.yaml sets
// WEATHER_ENABLED=false, so there is never a forecast: predictions use the prior difficulty.
async function shooterId(page: Page, name: string): Promise<number> {
  const response = await page.request.get(`/api/shooters?q=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
  expect(response.ok()).toBe(true);
  const rows = (await response.json()) as { shooter_id: number; display_name: string }[];
  const row = rows.find((r) => r.display_name === name);
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`${name} is not in the seeded fixtures`);
  return row.shooter_id;
}

test('home shows the Next Sunday card without a forecast', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  const card = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Next Sunday', exact: true });
  await expect(
    card.getByText('No forecast yet. It appears a few days before the shoot.'),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await expect(card.getByText('Predicted field median')).toBeVisible();
  const odds = card.getByRole('region', { name: 'Win odds' });
  await expect(odds.getByRole('button', { name: 'Table' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(odds.getByRole('button', { name: 'CSV' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('a regular sees an expected score on their profile', async ({ page }) => {
  const hadley = await shooterId(page, 'Hadley, Ike');
  await page.goto(`/shooters/${hadley}`);
  const outlook = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Next Sunday outlook' });
  await expect(outlook.getByText('Expected score')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(outlook.getByText('Chance of shooting')).toBeVisible();
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  conditionLabel,
  difficultyNote,
  forecastSummary,
  formatDay,
  formatPercent,
  formatRange,
  formatScore,
} from './format';
import { FORECAST, NEXT_PREDICTIONS } from './mocks';

describe('prediction formatting', () => {
  it('formats calendar days without a timezone shift', () => {
    expect(formatDay('2026-10-04')).toBe('Sun, Oct 4');
  });

  it('formats percentages, keeping tiny chances visible', () => {
    expect(formatPercent(0.372)).toBe('37%');
    expect(formatPercent(0.004)).toBe('<1%');
    expect(formatPercent(0)).toBe('0%');
    expect(formatPercent(1)).toBe('100%');
  });

  it('formats scores and a clipped likely range', () => {
    expect(formatScore(36.44)).toBe('36.4');
    expect(formatRange(38.6, 4.2)).toBe('34.4–42.8');
    expect(formatRange(48, 4)).toBe('44.0–50.0');
    expect(formatRange(2, 4)).toBe('0.0–6.0');
  });

  it('summarises the forecast window', () => {
    expect(forecastSummary(FORECAST)).toBe('54°F · gusts to 12 mph · 0.02 in rain · Rain');
    expect(forecastSummary({ ...FORECAST, precip_in: 0, condition: 'partly_cloudy' })).toBe(
      '54°F · gusts to 12 mph · dry · Partly cloudy',
    );
    expect(conditionLabel('hail')).toBe('hail');
  });

  it('explains the day difficulty and its source', () => {
    expect(difficultyNote(NEXT_PREDICTIONS)).toBe(
      'About 1.2 targets harder than a typical Sunday, going by the forecast.',
    );
    expect(difficultyNote({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, difficulty: -0.8 })).toBe(
      'About 0.8 targets easier than a typical Sunday, going by the forecast.',
    );
    expect(difficultyNote({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, difficulty: 0.02 })).toBe(
      'A typical Sunday for scores, going by the forecast.',
    );
    expect(
      difficultyNote({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, difficulty_source: 'intercept', difficulty: 0.1 }),
    ).toBe('No usable forecast yet, so this assumes a typical Sunday.');
    expect(
      difficultyNote({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, difficulty_source: 'prior', difficulty: null }),
    ).toBe('No weather model yet, so this assumes a typical Sunday.');
  });
});
```

Create `frontend/src/features/predictions/format.ts`:
```ts
import type { NextPredictions, PredictionForecast } from './api';

const DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  weekday: 'short',
  month: 'short',
  day: 'numeric',
  timeZone: 'UTC',
});

/** "2026-10-04" -> "Sun, Oct 4" (calendar dates carry no timezone). */
export function formatDay(iso: string): string {
  return DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}

/** 0.372 -> "37%"; anything above 0 but under 0.5% -> "<1%". */
export function formatPercent(p: number): string {
  if (p > 0 && p < 0.005) return '<1%';
  return `${Math.round(p * 100)}%`;
}

export function formatScore(value: number): string {
  return value.toFixed(1);
}

/** The likely range expected ± one standard deviation, clipped to 0..50. */
export function formatRange(expected: number, sd: number): string {
  const lo = Math.max(0, expected - sd);
  const hi = Math.min(50, expected + sd);
  return `${formatScore(lo)}–${formatScore(hi)}`;
}

const CONDITIONS: Record<string, string> = {
  rain: 'Rain',
  windy: 'Windy',
  overcast: 'Overcast',
  partly_cloudy: 'Partly cloudy',
  clear: 'Clear',
};

export function conditionLabel(condition: string): string {
  return CONDITIONS[condition] ?? condition;
}

/** "54°F · gusts to 12 mph · 0.02 in rain · Overcast" for the 10:00–12:00 window. */
export function forecastSummary(forecast: PredictionForecast): string {
  const parts = [
    `${Math.round(forecast.temp_f)}°F`,
    `gusts to ${Math.round(forecast.gust_mph)} mph`,
    forecast.precip_in > 0 ? `${forecast.precip_in.toFixed(2)} in rain` : 'dry',
    conditionLabel(forecast.condition),
  ];
  return parts.join(' · ');
}

/** Plain-Quigley day difficulty (positive = harder, C7) and where it came from. */
export function difficultyNote(p: NextPredictions): string {
  if (p.difficulty_source === 'weather' && p.difficulty !== null) {
    const size = Math.abs(p.difficulty);
    if (size < 0.05) return 'A typical Sunday for scores, going by the forecast.';
    const way = p.difficulty > 0 ? 'harder' : 'easier';
    return `About ${size.toFixed(1)} targets ${way} than a typical Sunday, going by the forecast.`;
  }
  if (p.difficulty_source === 'intercept') {
    return 'No usable forecast yet, so this assumes a typical Sunday.';
  }
  return 'No weather model yet, so this assumes a typical Sunday.';
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/predictions/charts.ts`:
```ts
import type { EChartsOption } from 'echarts';
import { BarChart } from 'echarts/charts';
import { GridComponent, TooltipComponent } from 'echarts/components';
import { use as registerECharts } from 'echarts/core';
import type { TabularData } from '../../components/charts/types';
import type { PredictedShooter } from './api';

registerECharts([BarChart, GridComponent, TooltipComponent]);

export const WIN_ODDS_TOP = 8;

const round1 = (value: number): number => Math.round(value * 10) / 10;

/**
 * Horizontal bars of the top shooters' chance to win next Sunday (win odds x chance of
 * attending, in %). `shooters` arrives sorted by that chance, best first.
 */
export function winOddsChart(
  shooters: PredictedShooter[],
  top: number = WIN_ODDS_TOP,
): { data: TabularData; option: EChartsOption } {
  const leaders = shooters.slice(0, top);
  const data: TabularData = {
    columns: [
      { key: 'shooter', label: 'Shooter', type: 'string' },
      { key: 'win_chance', label: 'Chance to win %', type: 'number' },
      { key: 'attend', label: 'Chance of attending %', type: 'number' },
      { key: 'expected', label: 'Expected score', type: 'number' },
    ],
    rows: leaders.map((s) => ({
      shooter: s.display_name,
      win_chance: round1(s.win_chance * 100),
      attend: round1(s.attend_prob * 100),
      expected: round1(s.expected),
    })),
  };
  const reversed = [...leaders].reverse();
  const option: EChartsOption = {
    grid: { left: 8, right: 16, top: 8, bottom: 8, containLabel: true },
    tooltip: { trigger: 'axis', valueFormatter: (value) => `${String(value)}%` },
    xAxis: { type: 'value', name: '%', min: 0 },
    yAxis: { type: 'category', data: reversed.map((s) => s.display_name) },
    series: [
      {
        type: 'bar',
        name: 'Chance to win',
        data: reversed.map((s) => round1(s.win_chance * 100)),
      },
    ],
  };
  return { data, option };
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { http, HttpResponse, type JsonBodyType } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { server } from '../../../test/msw/server';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../../test/render';
import { NEXT_PREDICTIONS } from '../mocks';
import { NextSundayCard } from './NextSundayCard';

function serve(body: JsonBodyType, status = 200) {
  server.use(http.get('*/api/predictions/next', () => HttpResponse.json(body, { status })));
}

describe('NextSundayCard', () => {
  it('shows the forecast, the predicted field and the win odds', async () => {
    serve(NEXT_PREDICTIONS);
    renderWithProviders(<NextSundayCard />);
    const card = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Next Sunday' });
    expect(await within(card).findByText('Sun, Oct 4')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      within(card).getByText('Forecast 10:00–12:00: 54°F · gusts to 12 mph · 0.02 in rain · Rain'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(card).getByText('36.4')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(card).getByText('About 27 shooters')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(card).getByText(/About 1.2 targets harder/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('every data chart exposes Table and CSV controls', async () => {
    serve(NEXT_PREDICTIONS);
    renderWithProviders(<NextSundayCard />);
    const chart = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Win odds' });
    expect(within(chart).getByRole('button', { name: 'Table' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(chart).getByRole('button', { name: 'CSV' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when there is no forecast yet', async () => {
    serve({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, forecast: null, difficulty_source: 'prior', difficulty: null });
    renderWithProviders(<NextSundayCard />);
    expect(
      await screen.findByText('No forecast yet. It appears a few days before the shoot.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('explains that predictions need an analytics run', async () => {
    serve({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, model_ready: false, field_median: null, shooters: [] });
    renderWithProviders(<NextSundayCard />);
    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Predictions appear after the first analytics run.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('region', { name: 'Win odds' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('handles an empty field and a missing median', async () => {
    serve({ ...NEXT_PREDICTIONS, field_median: null, expected_turnout: 0, shooters: [] });
    renderWithProviders(<NextSundayCard />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Nobody has shot in the last 13 Sundays.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('—')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reports a failed request', async () => {
    serve({ error: { code: 'internal', message: 'Internal server error' } }, 500);
    renderWithProviders(<NextSundayCard />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Could not load predictions.');
  });
});
```

Replace the whole content of `frontend/src/features/predictions/api.ts` (adds `useNextPredictions`):
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../../api/client';
import type { components } from '../../api/schema';

export type NextPredictions = components['schemas']['NextPredictionsOut'];
export type PredictedShooter = components['schemas']['PredictionShooterOut'];
export type PredictionForecast = components['schemas']['PredictionForecastOut'];

/** Next Sunday's predictions (C8 `GET /api/predictions/next`); never filtered (C7). */
export function useNextPredictions() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/predictions/next'],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/predictions/next')),
  });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/predictions/components/NextSundayCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId } from 'react';
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import { useNextPredictions, type NextPredictions } from '../api';
import { winOddsChart } from '../charts';
import { difficultyNote, forecastSummary, formatDay, formatScore } from '../format';

function Body({ data }: { data: NextPredictions }) {
  const chart = winOddsChart(data.shooters);
  return (
    <>
      <p className="text-text-muted">{formatDay(data.target_date)}</p>
      {data.forecast === null ? (
        <p>No forecast yet. It appears a few days before the shoot.</p>
      ) : (
        <p>Forecast 10:00–12:00: {forecastSummary(data.forecast)}</p>
      )}
      {data.model_ready ? (
        <>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <dt className="text-text-muted">Predicted field median</dt>
            <dd>{data.field_median === null ? '—' : formatScore(data.field_median)}</dd>
            <dt className="text-text-muted">Expected turnout</dt>
            <dd>About {Math.round(data.expected_turnout)} shooters</dd>
          </dl>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">{difficultyNote(data)}</p>
          {data.shooters.length === 0 ? (
            <p>Nobody has shot in the last 13 Sundays.</p>
          ) : (
            <ChartFrame
              title="Win odds"
              subtitle="Chance to win next Sunday, including the chance of showing up"
              option={chart.option}
              columns={chart.data.columns}
              rows={chart.data.rows}
              csvName="next-sunday-win-odds"
              ariaLabel="Bar chart of each regular's chance to win next Sunday"
              urlKey="next-win"
              zoom="none"
            />
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <p>Predictions appear after the first analytics run.</p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

/** Home "Next Sunday" card (C10 home widget, slot `main`). */
export function NextSundayCard() {
  const titleId = useId();
  const query = useNextPredictions();
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={titleId} className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-card bg-elevated p-4">
      <h2 id={titleId} className="text-lg font-medium">
        Next Sunday
      </h2>
      {query.isPending ? (
        <p role="status">Loading predictions…</p>
      ) : query.isError ? (
        <p role="alert">Could not load predictions.</p>
      ) : (
        <Body data={query.data} />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/predictions/components/ProfileOutlook.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useNextPredictions, type NextPredictions } from '../api';
import { formatDay, formatPercent, formatRange, formatScore } from '../format';

function Outlook({ data, shooterId }: { data: NextPredictions; shooterId: number }) {
  if (!data.model_ready) return <p>Predictions appear after the first analytics run.</p>;
  const row = data.shooters.find((s) => s.shooter_id === shooterId);
  if (row === undefined) {
    return (
      <p>Not a regular lately, so there is no expectation for {formatDay(data.target_date)}.</p>
    );
  }
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
      <dt className="text-text-muted">Expected score</dt>
      <dd>
        {formatScore(row.expected)} (likely {formatRange(row.expected, row.sd)})
      </dd>
      <dt className="text-text-muted">Chance of shooting</dt>
      <dd>{formatPercent(row.attend_prob)}</dd>
      <dt className="text-text-muted">Chance to win</dt>
      <dd>
        {formatPercent(row.win_chance)} ({formatPercent(row.p_win)} if there)
      </dd>
      <dt className="text-text-muted">Podium chance if there</dt>
      <dd>{formatPercent(row.p_podium)}</dd>
    </dl>
  );
}

/** Profile section: this shooter's expectation for next Sunday (C10 profile section). */
export function ProfileOutlook({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const query = useNextPredictions();
  return (
    <section aria-label="Next Sunday outlook" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {query.isPending ? (
        <p role="status">Loading predictions…</p>
      ) : query.isError ? (
        <p role="alert">Could not load predictions.</p>
      ) : (
        <Outlook data={query.data} shooterId={shooterId} />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- Test: `frontend/e2e/weather.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 06 / C7): `sunday_clays.analytics.frames`: `load_rounds(session) -> pd.DataFrame` (C7 columns incl.
  `event_date, shooter_id, display_name, score, round_type, residual` and the weather columns `temp_f, gust_mph,
  precip_in`), `temp_band(temp_f)`, `wind_band(gust_mph)`, `precip_band(precip_in)` (labels `<40`, `40-55`, `55-70`,
  `70-85`, `85+`; `<10`, `10-20`, `20+`; `dry`, `wet`), `apply_round_type_filter(df, round_types:
  Sequence[RoundType]) -> pd.DataFrame` (empty = no filter; filters the `round_type` column);
  `sunday_clays.analytics.cache.cached_by_data_version` (arguments after the session must be hashable);
  `sunday_clays.domain.round_type.RoundType`; `sunday_clays.api.routes._filters.round_type_param` (`Query(default=[],
  alias='round_type')`, typed `list[RoundType]`); `sunday_clays.analytics.skill.SkillParams` and
  `run_skill_model(rounds, params) -> SkillResult` (input columns `round_id, event_date, shooter_id, name_key,
  ordinal, score, held`; `SkillResult.events` has `event_date, difficulty`) — unit tests only; `db.get_session`.
- Consumes (C4 tables, read with `text()` SQL): `events(event_date, round_type, head_count, has_scores,
  results_complete)`, `event_metrics(event_date, n, median, mean, top_score, difficulty)`, `event_weather(event_date,
  temp_f, apparent_f, precip_in, wind_mph, gust_mph, wind_dir_deg, cloud_pct, humidity_pct, pressure_hpa, condition,
  source)`, `shooter_aliases(name_key, shooter_id)` (tests).
- Consumes (Plan 07, Decision 3): `api`, `unwrap`; `components`; `useRoundTypes()` →
  `[RoundTypeValue[], setter]`; `useUrlState(key, codec, default)`, `enumCodec(values)`, `intCodec`, `rangeCodec`
  (`lo..hi`); `NavItem = { label; path; icon; order; mobileTab? }`; `ChartFrame` (C10 props incl. `actions?:
  ReactNode`; a region named `title` with `Table` and `CSV` buttons); `TabularData`; `renderWithProviders(ui, { route?,
  path? })`; `server`; e2e `test`/`expect`.
- Consumes (Plan 08 T2a): `ProfileSection = { id; title; order; Component: FC<{ shooterId: number }> }`.
- Produces (consumed by Task 1): `sunday_clays.analytics.weather_effects`: `COVARIATES = ("temp_f", "gust_mph",
  "precip_in", "cloud_pct")`; `@dataclass(frozen=True) class ClubModel(covariates, coef, se, sigma2, n_events, means)`
  with `predict(x: Mapping[str, float]) -> float`; `fit_club_model(events, covariates=COVARIATES) -> ClubModel |
  None`; `@cached_by_data_version club_regression(session, round_types: tuple[RoundType, ...] = (), covariates:
  tuple[str, ...] = COVARIATES) -> ClubModel | None`; also `effects`, `sensitivity`, `turnout_stats`,
  `weather_events`, `load_weather_events` (all `@cached_by_data_version`).
- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/weather/events` → `WeatherEventOut[]` (`event_date, round_type, head_count,
  has_scores, n_rounds, median, mean, top_score, difficulty, temp_f, apparent_f, precip_in, wind_mph, gust_mph,
  wind_dir_deg, cloud_pct, humidity_pct, pressure_hpa, condition, temp_band, wind_band, precip_band`);
  `GET /api/weather/effects?round_type=` → `WeatherEffectsOut { model: WeatherModelOut | null; n_events; bands:
  WeatherBandOut[] }`, `WeatherModelOut { terms: { name, coef, se, mean }[]; sigma2; n_events }` (first term
  `intercept` with `mean: null`), `WeatherBandOut { dimension, band, n_events, n_rounds, mean_score, mean_difficulty
  }`; `GET /api/weather/sensitivity` → `WeatherSensitivityOut { scales: { covariate, mean, sd }[]; tau2: { covariate,
  tau2 }[]; shooters: { shooter_id, display_name, n_rounds, terms: { covariate, beta, se, shrunk, per_unit }[] }[] }`;
  `GET /api/weather/turnout` → `WeatherTurnoutOut[]` (`dimension, band, n_events, mean_head_count,
  median_head_count`). Frontend: route `/weather`, nav `{ label: 'Weather', path: '/weather', icon: CloudSun, order:
  90 }`, `profileSection` (`id: 'weather'`, `title: 'Weather sensitivity'`, `order: 50`), MSW default handlers for the
  four endpoints.

**Branch:** `task/11-2-weather-effects`

**Depends on:** Plan 05 T3 (`event_weather` rows are written by s20); Plan 06 T1 (`frames`, `cache`, `_filters`), T2
(`event_metrics`), T3 (`skill.py`), T4 (s30 difficulty and residuals); Plan 07 T4 (client, `useUrlState`,
`useRoundTypes`), T5, T6 (`ChartFrame`); Plan 08 T2a (profile section glob); Plan 04 T2 (e2e fixture seed).

**Working rules for this task:**
- Backend integration tests need Docker (testcontainers starts `postgres:17`) or `TEST_DATABASE_URL`. Unit tests never
  touch the DB. `pytest` runs with `filterwarnings = error`.
- `fx_session`/`fx_viewer_client` are the committed fixture world (both workbooks imported, pipeline run). It has no
  `event_weather` rows (no network in tests), so each weather test deletes and inserts its own rows inside its
  savepoint, which is rolled back at teardown. Never run the worker from these tests.
- An autouse fixture clears the `@cached_by_data_version` memo before each test.
- Fixture facts used below (pandas over the fixture workbooks): round counts 2026-08-16 48, 08-23 29, 08-30 36,
  09-06 24 (super sporting, 884 clays broken), 09-13 13 (super sporting, 448), 09-27 23 (900); 2018-12-30 is
  attendance-only with head count 7; 84 held events from 2025-01-01 on, where 53 shooters have ≥10 rounds and Hadley,
  Ike has 78.
- Frontend: Vitest + Testing Library + MSW; every feature's `mocks.ts` `handlers` are merged into the global server;
  a test overrides a response with `server.use(...)`. Weather surfaces are covered by Vitest + MSW; e2e only checks
  their empty state because compose.test.yaml sets `WEATHER_ENABLED=false`.
