```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { server } from '../../../test/msw/server';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../../test/render';
import { WEATHER_EFFECTS, WEATHER_EVENTS, WEATHER_SENSITIVITY, WEATHER_TURNOUT } from '../mocks';
import { WeatherPage } from './WeatherPage';

const CHARTS = [
  'Difficulty and weather',
  'Wind rose',
  'Scores by conditions',
  'Turnout by conditions',
  'Weather sensitivity',
];

function serveAll(effectsUrls: string[] = []) {
  server.use(
    http.get('*/api/weather/events', () => HttpResponse.json(WEATHER_EVENTS)),
    http.get('*/api/weather/effects', ({ request }) => {
      effectsUrls.push(request.url);
      return HttpResponse.json(WEATHER_EFFECTS);
    }),
    http.get('*/api/weather/sensitivity', () => HttpResponse.json(WEATHER_SENSITIVITY)),
    http.get('*/api/weather/turnout', () => HttpResponse.json(WEATHER_TURNOUT)),
  );
}

describe('WeatherPage', () => {
  it('every data chart exposes Table and CSV controls', async () => {
    serveAll();
    renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />);
    for (const title of CHARTS) {
      const chart = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: title });
      expect(within(chart).getByRole('button', { name: 'Table' })).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(within(chart).getByRole('button', { name: 'CSV' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
    expect(screen.getByText(/club model over 84 Sundays/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the empty state before any Sunday has weather', async () => {
    server.use(http.get('*/api/weather/events', () => HttpResponse.json([])));
    renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'No weather data yet' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('region', { name: 'Wind rose' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('applies the global round-type filter to events and the effects request', async () => {
    const urls: string[] = [];
    serveAll(urls);
    renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />, { route: '/?rt=super_sporting' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('1 of 1 scored Sundays match.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Scores by conditions' });
    expect(new URL(urls[0] ?? '').searchParams.getAll('round_type')).toEqual(['super_sporting']);
  });

  it('switches the grouping of the band charts and keeps it in the URL', async () => {
    serveAll();
    const { user, router } = renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />);
    const scores = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Scores by conditions' });
    await user.selectOptions(
      within(scores).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Group by' }),
      'condition',
    );
    expect(router.state.location.search).toBe('?wdim=condition');
    await user.click(within(scores).getByRole('button', { name: 'Table' }));
    expect(within(scores).getByRole('cell', { name: 'Rain' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const fit = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Difficulty and weather' });
    await user.selectOptions(
      within(fit).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Weather measure' }),
      'temp_f',
    );
    const sensitivity = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Weather sensitivity' });
    await user.selectOptions(
      within(sensitivity).getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Sensitivity to' }),
      'precip_in',
    );
    // ChartFrame keeps its Table toggle in the URL too (C10: useUrlState(urlKey)), so the
    // click above added wb=table between the two selects.
    expect(router.state.location.search).toBe('?wdim=condition&wb=table&wx=temp_f&wsc=precip_in');
  });

  it('explains a missing model', async () => {
    serveAll();
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/weather/effects', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ ...WEATHER_EFFECTS, model: null }),
      ),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />);
    expect(
      await screen.findByText(/Not enough Sundays with weather for a club model yet/),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reports failed requests per chart and for the page', async () => {
    const failed = () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'internal', message: 'x' } }, { status: 500 });
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/weather/events', () => HttpResponse.json(WEATHER_EVENTS)),
      http.get('*/api/weather/effects', failed),
      http.get('*/api/weather/sensitivity', failed),
      http.get('*/api/weather/turnout', failed),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Could not load weather effects.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Could not load turnout.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Could not load weather sensitivity.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reports a failed events request', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/weather/events', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'internal', message: 'x' } }, { status: 500 }),
      ),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<WeatherPage />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Could not load weather.');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from 'react-router';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { profileSection } from './profileSection';
import { nav, routes } from './routes';

describe('weather feature registration', () => {
  it('serves the lazy weather page at /weather with its nav item', async () => {
    // A parent route with a HydrateFallback, as in the app's layout route (and Plan 08's route
    // tests), so the lazy child loads without React Router's "no HydrateFallback" warning.
    const router = createMemoryRouter(
      [{ path: '/', HydrateFallback: () => null, children: routes }],
      { initialEntries: ['/weather'] },
    );
    render(
      <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient()}>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </QueryClientProvider>,
    );
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Weather', level: 1 })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Scores by conditions' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Turnout by conditions' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Weather sensitivity' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(nav).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ path: '/weather', order: 90 })]);
  });

  it('adds the weather sensitivity section to profiles', async () => {
    render(
      <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient()}>
        <profileSection.Component shooterId={59} />
      </QueryClientProvider>,
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Wind gusts: −0.6 targets/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

Create `frontend/src/features/weather/pages/WeatherPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import { useRoundTypes } from '../../../lib/roundTypes';
import { enumCodec, useUrlState } from '../../../lib/useUrlState';
import {
  useWeatherEffects,
  useWeatherEvents,
  useWeatherSensitivity,
  useWeatherTurnout,
  type WeatherEvent,
} from '../api';
import {
  bandScoreChart,
  difficultyFitChart,
  sensitivityChart,
  turnoutChart,
  windRoseChart,
} from '../charts';
import { ConditionsExplorer } from '../components/ConditionsExplorer';
import {
  COVARIATE_LABELS,
  DIMENSIONS,
  DIMENSION_LABELS,
  MODEL_COVARIATES,
  SENSITIVITY_COVARIATES,
  type Dimension,
  type ModelCovariate,
  type SensitivityCovariate,
} from '../format';

const covariateCodec = enumCodec(MODEL_COVARIATES);
const dimensionCodec = enumCodec(DIMENSIONS);
const sensitivityCodec = enumCodec(SENSITIVITY_COVARIATES);

function Pending({ isError, what }: { isError: boolean; what: string }) {
  return isError ? (
    <p role="alert">Could not load {what}.</p>
  ) : (
    <p role="status">Loading {what}…</p>
  );
}

function Picker<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  labels,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: T;
  options: readonly T[];
  labels: Record<T, string>;
  onChange: (next: T) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <span className="sr-only">{label}</span>
      <select
        aria-label={label}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value as T)}
        className="min-h-11 rounded-button bg-surface px-3"
      >
        {options.map((o) => (
          <option key={o} value={o}>
            {labels[o]}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </label>
  );
}

const SENSITIVITY_LABELS: Record<SensitivityCovariate, string> = {
  temp_f: 'Per 10 °F warmer',
  gust_mph: 'Per 10 mph more gust',
  precip_in: 'Per 0.1 in more rain',
};

function WeatherCharts({ events }: { events: WeatherEvent[] }) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  const effects = useWeatherEffects();
  const sensitivity = useWeatherSensitivity();
  const turnout = useWeatherTurnout();
  const [covariate, setCovariate] = useUrlState<ModelCovariate>('wx', covariateCodec, 'gust_mph');
  const [dimension, setDimension] = useUrlState<Dimension>('wdim', dimensionCodec, 'temp_band');
  const [sensitivityCovariate, setSensitivityCovariate] = useUrlState<SensitivityCovariate>(
    'wsc',
    sensitivityCodec,
    'gust_mph',
  );
  const shown =
    roundTypes.length === 0
      ? events
      : events.filter((e) => roundTypes.some((rt) => rt === e.round_type));
  const fit = difficultyFitChart(shown, effects.data?.model ?? null, covariate);
  const rose = windRoseChart(shown);
  const dimensionPicker = (
    <Picker
      label="Group by"
      value={dimension}
      options={DIMENSIONS}
      labels={DIMENSION_LABELS}
      onChange={setDimension}
    />
  );
  return (
    <>
      <ConditionsExplorer events={shown} />
      <ChartFrame
        title="Difficulty and weather"
        subtitle={
          effects.data?.model
            ? `Line: club model over ${effects.data.model.n_events} Sundays (positive = harder)`
            : 'Not enough Sundays with weather for a club model yet (positive = harder)'
        }
        option={fit.option}
        columns={fit.data.columns}
        rows={fit.data.rows}
        csvName="weather-difficulty"
        ariaLabel="Scatter plot of event difficulty against the chosen weather measure"
        urlKey="wf"
        zoom="xy"
        actions={
          <Picker
            label="Weather measure"
            value={covariate}
            options={MODEL_COVARIATES}
            labels={COVARIATE_LABELS}
            onChange={setCovariate}
          />
        }
      />
      <ChartFrame
        title="Wind rose"
        subtitle="Mean field median by the direction the wind came from"
        option={rose.option}
        columns={rose.data.columns}
        rows={rose.data.rows}
        csvName="weather-wind-rose"
        ariaLabel="Polar bar chart of mean field median by wind direction"
        urlKey="wr"
        zoom="none"
      />
      {effects.data ? (
        <ChartFrame
          title="Scores by conditions"
          subtitle="Mean score of every round shot in each band"
          {...chartProps(bandScoreChart(effects.data.bands, dimension))}
          csvName="weather-scores-by-band"
          ariaLabel="Bar chart of mean score per weather band"
          urlKey="wb"
          actions={dimensionPicker}
        />
      ) : (
        <Pending isError={effects.isError} what="weather effects" />
      )}
      {turnout.data ? (
        <ChartFrame
          title="Turnout by conditions"
          subtitle="Mean head count in each band"
          {...chartProps(turnoutChart(turnout.data, dimension))}
          csvName="weather-turnout"
          ariaLabel="Bar chart of mean head count per weather band"
          urlKey="wt"
          actions={dimensionPicker}
        />
      ) : (
        <Pending isError={turnout.isError} what="turnout" />
      )}
      {sensitivity.data ? (
        <ChartFrame
          title="Weather sensitivity"
          subtitle="Change in a shooter's score beyond the day's difficulty, pulled toward the club"
          {...chartProps(sensitivityChart(sensitivity.data.shooters, sensitivityCovariate))}
          csvName="weather-sensitivity"
          ariaLabel="Bar chart of each shooter's weather sensitivity"
          urlKey="ws"
          actions={
            <Picker
              label="Sensitivity to"
              value={sensitivityCovariate}
              options={SENSITIVITY_COVARIATES}
              labels={SENSITIVITY_LABELS}
              onChange={setSensitivityCovariate}
            />
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <Pending isError={sensitivity.isError} what="weather sensitivity" />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function chartProps(chart: ReturnType<typeof bandScoreChart>) {
  return { option: chart.option, columns: chart.data.columns, rows: chart.data.rows };
}

export function WeatherPage() {
  const events = useWeatherEvents();
  return (
    <main className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Weather</h1>
      {events.isPending || events.isError ? (
        <Pending isError={events.isError} what="weather" />
      ) : events.data.length === 0 ? (
        <section aria-label="No weather data yet" className="rounded-card bg-elevated p-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">No weather data yet</h2>
          <p className="text-text-muted">
            Weather for each Sunday is fetched after the shoot. These charts appear once events have
            weather.
          </p>
        </section>
      ) : (
        <WeatherCharts events={events.data} />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
```

### Task 3: Compare, head-to-head and automatic rivals (master Plan 11 T3)

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/sunday_clays/analytics/rivals.py`
- Create: `backend/src/sunday_clays/api/routes/compare.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/extras/test_extras_rivals.py`, `backend/tests/integration/extras/test_extras_compare_routes.py`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/compare/api.ts`, `format.ts`, `charts.ts`, `mocks.ts`, `routes.tsx`, `profileSection.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/compare/components/ShooterPicker.tsx`, `components/RivalsSection.tsx`, `components/CompareTable.tsx`, `components/HeadToHeadCard.tsx`, `pages/ComparePage.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/src/features/compare/format.test.ts`, `charts.test.ts`, `routes.test.tsx`, `components/ShooterPicker.test.tsx`, `components/RivalsSection.test.tsx`, `pages/ComparePage.test.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/e2e/compare.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 06 / C7): `sunday_clays.analytics.frames.load_rounds(session)` (C7 columns incl. `event_date,
  shooter_id, score, round_type, event_rank, is_best_round, mu_before`), `load_rating_history(session)` (`shooter_id,
  event_date, mu, var`), `apply_round_type_filter(df, round_types)` (empty = no filter); the C7 best-round rule
  (`is_best_round`: exactly one per (event_date, shooter_id); `event_rank` = full-field min-rank of best rounds);
  `cache.cached_by_data_version`; `domain.round_type.RoundType`; `api.routes._filters.round_type_param`;
  `domain.errors.DomainError(code, message)` (400) and `NotFoundError(code, message)` (404), rendered as
  `{"error": {"code", "message"}}`; `db.get_session`; C4 `shooter_profiles(shooter_id, display_name)` and
  `shooter_aliases(name_key, shooter_id)` (tests).
- Consumes (Plan 06 HTTP, Decision 2): `GET /api/shooters` → `{ shooter_id, display_name, … }[]` (the picker reads
  only those two fields).
- Consumes (Plan 07, Decision 3): `api`, `unwrap`; `components`, `paths`; `useRoundTypes()`; `useUrlState`, `intCodec`,
  `listCodec` (comma list, duplicates and invalid items dropped); `NavItem`; `ChartFrame`; `TabularData`;
  `renderWithProviders(ui, { route?, path? })`; `server`; e2e `test`/`expect`.
- Consumes (Plan 08 T2a): `ProfileSection = { id; title; order; Component: FC<{ shooterId: number }> }`.
- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/compare?ids=<id>&ids=<id>[&round_type=]` → `CompareOut { shooters: CompareShooterOut[];
  pairs: ComparePairOut[]; rating: CompareRatingPointOut[] }` with `CompareShooterOut { shooter_id, display_name,
  n_rounds, n_events, avg_score, median_score, best_score, wins, podiums, current_mu, peak_mu, peak_date }`,
  `ComparePairOut { a, b, meetings, a_wins, b_wins, ties }`, `CompareRatingPointOut { shooter_id, event_date, mu }`
  (400 `invalid_compare` unless 2–4 distinct ids; 404 `shooter_not_found`); `GET /api/h2h?a=&b=[&round_type=]` →
  `H2HOut { a: { shooter_id, display_name }; b: …; meetings: { event_date, a_score, b_score, a_rank, b_rank, result:
  'a' | 'b' | 'tie' }[]; a_wins; b_wins; ties; avg_margin }` (400 `invalid_h2h` when `a == b`); `GET
  /api/shooters/{id}/rivals` → `RivalsOut { shooter_id; rivals: RivalOut[]; nemesis: RivalOut | null }` with
  `RivalOut { shooter_id, display_name, meetings, wins, losses, ties, mean_mu_gap, rivalry }`. Python:
  `sunday_clays.analytics.rivals.best_rounds(rounds)`, `head_to_head(best, a, b) -> HeadToHead`, `rivals(best,
  shooter_id, names) -> tuple[list[Rival], Rival | None]`. Frontend: route `/compare` (URL key `ids`, comma list), nav
  `{ label: 'Compare', path: '/compare', icon: Scale, order: 100 }`, `profileSection` (`id: 'rivals'`, `title:
  'Rivals'`, `order: 40`), `compareHref(ids) -> '/compare?ids=1,2'`, MSW default handlers for `/api/compare`,
  `/api/h2h` and `/api/shooters/:id/rivals`, and the non-default `directoryHandler` for `/api/shooters`.

**Branch:** `task/11-3-compare-rivals`

**Depends on:** Plan 06 T1 (`frames`, `cache`, `_filters`), T2 (best round, ranks), T4 (`mu_before`,
`rating_history`), T6 (`GET /api/shooters`); Plan 07 T4 (client, `useUrlState`, `useRoundTypes`), T5, T6
(`ChartFrame`); Plan 08 T2a (profile section glob); Plan 04 T2 (e2e fixture seed).

**Working rules for this task:**
- Backend integration tests need Docker (testcontainers starts `postgres:17`) or `TEST_DATABASE_URL`. Unit tests never
  touch the DB. `pytest` runs with `filterwarnings = error`.
- `fx_session`/`fx_viewer_client` are the committed fixture world (both workbooks imported, pipeline run); tests only
  read it here.
- An autouse fixture clears the `@cached_by_data_version` memo before each test.
- Fixture facts (pandas over the fixture workbooks, best round per shooter and day): Hadley, Ike vs Abernathy, Preston =
  249 meetings, 53–177–19, average margin −2.9759036, first 2020-01-05; with `round_type=super_sporting` only
  2026-09-06 (33 vs 40) and 2026-09-13 (34 vs 41); every other fixture day is `unknown` (no station data), so none is
  plain `sporting`. Hadley–Kaplan, Noel 209 meetings, 66–132–11; Abernathy–Kaplan 222, 111–95–16. Rounds / events /
  median / best / wins / podiums / average: Hadley 267 / 267 / 36 / 45 / 2 / 14 / 35.3071; Abernathy 286 / 285 / 38 /
  47 / 8 / 51 / 38.2483; Kaplan 242 / 240 / 37.5 / 45 / 4 / 28 / 37.1033. Super sporting only: Hadley 2 rounds, average
  33.5, best 34; Abernathy 2, 40.5, 41, one podium; Kaplan 2, 33.5, 36. Hadley's nemesis is Fullerton, Tate: 173
  meetings, 10 wins, 160 losses, 3 ties.
- Frontend: Vitest + Testing Library + MSW; every feature's `mocks.ts` `handlers` are merged into the global server;
  handlers for another plan's endpoint (`/api/shooters`) are never default handlers here — tests install
  `directoryHandler` with `server.use(...)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing unit tests for head-to-head, compare stats and rivals**

Create `backend/tests/unit/extras/test_extras_rivals.py`:
```python
"""Plan 11 Task 3: pure compare, head-to-head and rival rules (no database)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import date, timedelta

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from sunday_clays.analytics.rivals import (
    CompareStats,
    Meeting,
    RatingPoint,
    best_rounds,
    compare_stats,
    head_to_head,
    prepare_rounds,
    rating_points,
    rivals,
)

D = [date(2025, 1, 5) + timedelta(days=7 * k) for k in range(60)]
NAMES = {
    1: "Able, Al",
    2: "Baker, Bo",
    3: "Slocum, Cy",
    4: "Dunn, Di",
    5: "Eads, Ed",
    6: "Fox, Fi",
    7: "Whitlock, Gus",
}


def _rounds(rows: list[tuple[date, int, int, bool, float, float]]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """rows: (event_date, shooter_id, score, is_best_round, event_rank, mu_before)."""
    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        rows,
        columns=["event_date", "shooter_id", "score", "is_best_round", "event_rank", "mu_before"],
    )
    frame["round_type"] = "unknown"
    return prepare_rounds(frame)


def test_head_to_head_uses_best_round_per_day() -> None:
    rounds = _rounds(
        [
            (D[0], 1, 38, False, math.nan, 30.0),  # shooter 1's second round that day
            (D[0], 1, 45, True, 1.0, 30.0),
            (D[0], 2, 40, True, 2.0, 31.0),
            (D[1], 1, 36, True, 2.0, 30.5),
            (D[1], 2, 36, True, 2.0, 31.0),
            (D[2], 1, 30, True, 3.0, 30.4),
            (D[2], 2, 41, True, 1.0, 31.2),
            (D[3], 2, 44, True, 1.0, 31.5),  # shooter 1 absent: not a meeting
        ]
    )
    result = head_to_head(best_rounds(rounds), 1, 2)
    assert result.meetings == (
        Meeting(D[0], 45, 40, 1, 2, "a"),
        Meeting(D[1], 36, 36, 2, 2, "tie"),
        Meeting(D[2], 30, 41, 3, 1, "b"),
    )
    assert (result.a_wins, result.b_wins, result.ties) == (1, 1, 1)
    assert result.avg_margin == pytest.approx((5 + 0 - 11) / 3)


def test_head_to_head_without_shared_events_is_empty() -> None:
    rounds = _rounds([(D[0], 1, 40, True, 1.0, 30.0), (D[1], 2, 41, True, 1.0, 30.0)])
    result = head_to_head(best_rounds(rounds), 1, 2)
    assert (result.meetings, result.a_wins, result.b_wins, result.ties, result.avg_margin) == (
        (),
        0,
        0,
        0,
        None,
    )


def test_prepare_rounds_normalises_loader_dtypes() -> None:
    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "event_date": pd.to_datetime(["2026-01-04", "2026-01-04"]),
            "shooter_id": [1, 2],
            "score": [40, 39],
            "is_best_round": [True, None],
            "event_rank": [1, None],
            "mu_before": [None, 31.5],
        }
    )
    out = prepare_rounds(frame)
    assert out["event_date"].tolist() == [date(2026, 1, 4), date(2026, 1, 4)]
    assert out["is_best_round"].tolist() == [True, False]
    assert out["event_rank"].iloc[0] == 1.0
    assert math.isnan(out["event_rank"].iloc[1])


def test_compare_stats_scores_from_rounds_and_ratings_from_history() -> None:
    rounds = _rounds(
        [
            (D[0], 1, 40, True, 1.0, 30.0),
            (D[0], 1, 44, False, math.nan, 30.0),
            (D[1], 1, 35, True, 3.0, 30.5),
            (D[2], 1, 30, True, 6.0, 30.2),
        ]
    )
    history = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "shooter_id": [1, 1, 1],
            "event_date": [D[0], D[1], D[2]],
            "mu": [31.0, 33.0, 32.0],
            "var": [4.0, 3.5, 3.4],
        }
    )
    (stats,) = compare_stats(rounds, history, [1], NAMES)
    assert stats == CompareStats(
        shooter_id=1,
        display_name="Able, Al",
        n_rounds=4,
        n_events=3,
        avg_score=37.25,
        median_score=37.5,
        best_score=44,
        wins=1,
        podiums=2,
        current_mu=32.0,
        peak_mu=33.0,
        peak_date=D[1],
    )


def test_compare_stats_for_shooter_without_rounds_or_ratings() -> None:
    rounds = _rounds([(D[0], 1, 40, True, 1.0, 30.0)])
    history = pd.DataFrame({"shooter_id": [1], "event_date": [D[0]], "mu": [31.0], "var": [4.0]})
    (stats,) = compare_stats(rounds, history, [2], NAMES)
    assert (stats.n_rounds, stats.avg_score, stats.median_score, stats.best_score) == (
        0,
        None,
        None,
        None,
    )
    assert (stats.wins, stats.podiums, stats.current_mu, stats.peak_mu, stats.peak_date) == (
        0,
        0,
        None,
        None,
        None,
    )


def test_rating_points_are_grouped_by_shooter_in_date_order() -> None:
    history = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "shooter_id": [2, 1, 1, 3],
            "event_date": [D[0], D[1], D[0], D[0]],
            "mu": [30.0, 31.5, 31.0, 29.0],
            "var": [1.0] * 4,
        }
    )
    assert rating_points(history, [1, 2]) == [
        RatingPoint(1, D[0], 31.0),
        RatingPoint(1, D[1], 31.5),
        RatingPoint(2, D[0], 30.0),
    ]


def _meetings(
    other: int, scores: list[tuple[int, int]], gap: float | None, start: int
) -> list[tuple[date, int, int, bool, float, float]]:
    """Shooter 1 meets `other` on D[start], D[start + 1], ... with (shooter 1, other) scores."""
    rows = []
    for k, (mine, theirs) in enumerate(scores):
        day = D[start + k]
        rows.append((day, 1, mine, True, math.nan, 30.0))
        rows.append((day, other, theirs, True, math.nan, math.nan if gap is None else 30.0 + gap))
    return rows


def test_rivals_rank_by_meetings_over_rating_gap_and_pick_nemesis() -> None:
    four = [(40, 41), (40, 39), (40, 40), (40, 42), (40, 45), (40, 41), (40, 41)]
    rows = (
        _meetings(2, [(40, 38)] * 6, gap=1.0, start=0)  # 6 meetings / (1 + 1) = 3.0; 6 wins
        + _meetings(3, [(30, 40)] * 8, gap=3.0, start=10)  # 8 / (1 + 3) = 2.0; 8 net losses
        + _meetings(4, four, gap=0.0, start=20)  # 7 / (1 + 0) = 7.0; 1 win, 5 losses, 1 tie
        + _meetings(5, [(40, 41)] * 4, gap=0.0, start=30)  # only 4 meetings: not a candidate
        + _meetings(6, [(40, 41)] * 8, gap=None, start=40)  # unrated: never a rival; 8 net losses
    )
    ranked, nemesis = rivals(best_rounds(_rounds(rows)), 1, NAMES)
    assert [(r.shooter_id, r.meetings, r.rivalry) for r in ranked] == [
        (4, 7, 7.0),
        (2, 6, 3.0),
        (3, 8, 2.0),
    ]
    assert (ranked[0].wins, ranked[0].losses, ranked[0].ties) == (1, 5, 1)
    assert ranked[0].mean_mu_gap == 0.0
    # Shooters 3 and 6 both have 8 net losses over 8 meetings -> display name decides (Slocum < Fox).
    assert nemesis is not None
    assert (nemesis.shooter_id, nemesis.losses - nemesis.wins, nemesis.rivalry) == (3, 8, 2.0)


def test_rivals_keep_the_top_five_and_can_have_no_nemesis() -> None:
    rows: list[tuple[date, int, int, bool, float, float]] = []
    for other in range(2, 8):  # rivalry 5 / (1 + other): 2 is the closest, 7 the farthest
        rows += _meetings(other, [(45, 30)] * 5, gap=float(other), start=5 * other)
    ranked, nemesis = rivals(best_rounds(_rounds(rows)), 1, NAMES)
    assert [r.shooter_id for r in ranked] == [2, 3, 4, 5, 6]
    assert nemesis is None


def test_rivals_for_shooter_without_meetings() -> None:
    frame = _rounds([(D[0], 1, 40, True, 1.0, 30.0)])
    assert rivals(best_rounds(frame), 1, NAMES) == ([], None)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `analytics/rivals.py`**

Create `backend/src/sunday_clays/analytics/rivals.py`:
```python
"""Shooter comparison, head-to-head on shared events, automatic rivals and nemesis."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from typing import Final, Literal

import pandas as pd
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from sunday_clays.analytics import frames
from sunday_clays.analytics.cache import cached_by_data_version

MIN_MEETINGS: Final = 5
TOP_RIVALS: Final = 5
BEST_COLUMNS: Final[tuple[str, ...]] = (
    "event_date",
    "shooter_id",
    "score",
    "event_rank",
    "mu_before",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Meeting:
    event_date: date
    a_score: int
    b_score: int
    a_rank: int
    b_rank: int
    result: Literal["a", "b", "tie"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HeadToHead:
    a: int
    b: int
    meetings: tuple[Meeting, ...]
    a_wins: int
    b_wins: int
    ties: int
    avg_margin: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CompareStats:
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str
    n_rounds: int
    n_events: int
    avg_score: float | None
    median_score: float | None
    best_score: int | None
    wins: int
    podiums: int
    current_mu: float | None
    peak_mu: float | None
    peak_date: date | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RatingPoint:
    shooter_id: int
    event_date: date
    mu: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rival:
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str
    meetings: int
    wins: int
    losses: int
    ties: int
    mean_mu_gap: float | None
    rivalry: float | None


def _as_dates(values: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
    return pd.Series(pd.to_datetime(values).dt.date, index=values.index, dtype=object)


def prepare_rounds(rounds: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Normalise Plan 06 `load_rounds` dtypes: dates, a plain-bool best flag, float ranks."""
    out = rounds.copy()
    out["event_date"] = _as_dates(out["event_date"])
    out["is_best_round"] = out["is_best_round"].astype("boolean").fillna(False).astype(bool)
    out["event_rank"] = pd.to_numeric(out["event_rank"], errors="coerce").astype(float)
    out["mu_before"] = pd.to_numeric(out["mu_before"], errors="coerce").astype(float)
    return out


def best_rounds(rounds: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One row per (event_date, shooter_id): the C7 best round, with its full-field rank."""
    return rounds.loc[rounds["is_best_round"], list(BEST_COLUMNS)]


def head_to_head(best: pd.DataFrame, a: int, b: int) -> HeadToHead:
    """Meetings = dates on which both shooters have a best round; equal scores are ties."""
    left = best.loc[best["shooter_id"] == a, ["event_date", "score", "event_rank"]]
    right = best.loc[best["shooter_id"] == b, ["event_date", "score", "event_rank"]]
    joined = left.merge(right, on="event_date", suffixes=("_a", "_b")).sort_values("event_date")
    meetings: list[Meeting] = []
    for rec in joined.to_dict("records"):
        a_score, b_score = int(rec["score_a"]), int(rec["score_b"])
        result: Literal["a", "b", "tie"] = (
            "a" if a_score > b_score else "b" if b_score > a_score else "tie"
        )
        meetings.append(
            Meeting(
                event_date=rec["event_date"],
                a_score=a_score,
                b_score=b_score,
                a_rank=int(rec["event_rank_a"]),
                b_rank=int(rec["event_rank_b"]),
                result=result,
            )
        )
    margins = [m.a_score - m.b_score for m in meetings]
    return HeadToHead(
        a=a,
        b=b,
        meetings=tuple(meetings),
        a_wins=sum(m.result == "a" for m in meetings),
        b_wins=sum(m.result == "b" for m in meetings),
        ties=sum(m.result == "tie" for m in meetings),
        avg_margin=sum(margins) / len(margins) if margins else None,
    )


def compare_stats(
    rounds: pd.DataFrame,
    history: pd.DataFrame,
    ids: Sequence[int],
    names: Mapping[int, str],
) -> list[CompareStats]:
    """Score stats over `rounds` (already filtered) and rating stats over the full `history`."""
    out: list[CompareStats] = []
    for shooter_id in ids:
        mine = rounds[rounds["shooter_id"] == shooter_id]
        best = mine[mine["is_best_round"]]
        scores = mine["score"].astype(float)
        ratings = history[history["shooter_id"] == shooter_id].sort_values("event_date")
        mus = [float(v) for v in ratings["mu"]]
        dates = list(ratings["event_date"])
        peak = max(range(len(mus)), key=mus.__getitem__) if mus else None
        out.append(
            CompareStats(
                shooter_id=shooter_id,
                display_name=names[shooter_id],
                n_rounds=len(mine),
                n_events=int(mine["event_date"].nunique()),
                avg_score=float(scores.mean()) if len(mine) else None,
                median_score=float(scores.median()) if len(mine) else None,
                best_score=int(scores.max()) if len(mine) else None,
                wins=int((best["event_rank"] == 1).sum()),
                podiums=int((best["event_rank"] <= 3).sum()),
                current_mu=mus[-1] if mus else None,
                peak_mu=None if peak is None else mus[peak],
                peak_date=None if peak is None else dates[peak],
            )
        )
    return out


def rating_points(history: pd.DataFrame, ids: Sequence[int]) -> list[RatingPoint]:
    rows = history[history["shooter_id"].isin(list(ids))].sort_values(["shooter_id", "event_date"])
    return [
        RatingPoint(int(r["shooter_id"]), r["event_date"], float(r["mu"]))
        for r in rows.to_dict("records")
    ]


def rivals(
    best: pd.DataFrame, shooter_id: int, names: Mapping[int, str]
) -> tuple[list[Rival], Rival | None]:
    """Top rivals (many meetings at a small rating gap) and the nemesis (most net losses)."""
    mine = best.loc[best["shooter_id"] == shooter_id, ["event_date", "score", "mu_before"]]
    others = best.loc[
        best["shooter_id"] != shooter_id, ["event_date", "shooter_id", "score", "mu_before"]
    ]
    joined = mine.merge(others, on="event_date", suffixes=("", "_o"))
    candidates: list[Rival] = []
    for _, group in joined.groupby("shooter_id", sort=True):
        if len(group) < MIN_MEETINGS:
            continue
        other = int(group["shooter_id"].iloc[0])
        gaps = (group["mu_before"] - group["mu_before_o"]).abs().dropna()
        gap = float(gaps.mean()) if len(gaps) else None
        candidates.append(
            Rival(
                shooter_id=other,
                display_name=names[other],
                meetings=len(group),
                wins=int((group["score"] > group["score_o"]).sum()),
                losses=int((group["score"] < group["score_o"]).sum()),
                ties=int((group["score"] == group["score_o"]).sum()),
                mean_mu_gap=gap,
                rivalry=None if gap is None else len(group) / (1.0 + gap),
            )
        )
    ranked = sorted(
        (c for c in candidates if c.rivalry is not None),
        key=lambda c: (-(c.rivalry or 0.0), -c.meetings, c.display_name, c.shooter_id),
    )
    nemeses = sorted(
        (c for c in candidates if c.losses > c.wins),
        key=lambda c: (-(c.losses - c.wins), -c.meetings, c.display_name, c.shooter_id),
    )
    return ranked[:TOP_RIVALS], nemeses[0] if nemeses else None


def display_names(session: Session) -> dict[int, str]:
    rows = session.execute(text("SELECT shooter_id, display_name FROM shooter_profiles")).all()
    return {int(r[0]): str(r[1]) for r in rows}


@cached_by_data_version
def compare_rounds(session: Session) -> pd.DataFrame:
    return prepare_rounds(frames.load_rounds(session))


@cached_by_data_version
def rating_history(session: Session) -> pd.DataFrame:
    history = frames.load_rating_history(session).copy()
    history["event_date"] = _as_dates(history["event_date"])
    return history
```

```python
"""Plan 11 Task 3: compare, head-to-head and rivals routes over the committed fixtures."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session


def _shooter_id(session: Session, name_key: str) -> int:
    return int(
        session.execute(
            text("SELECT shooter_id FROM shooter_aliases WHERE name_key = :k"), {"k": name_key}
        ).scalar_one()
    )


def _ok(client: TestClient, url: str, params: Any = None) -> Any:
    response = client.get(url, params=params)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()


def _error(client: TestClient, url: str, params: Any, status: int) -> str:
    response = client.get(url, params=params)
    assert response.status_code == status, response.text
    code: str = response.json()["error"]["code"] if status != 422 else "validation"
    return code


@pytest.fixture
def ids(fx_session: Session) -> dict[str, int]:
    keys = {"hadley": "hadley ike", "preston": "abernathy preston", "noel": "kaplan noel"}
    return {k: _shooter_id(fx_session, v) for k, v in keys.items()}


def test_head_to_head_hadley_vs_abernathy(fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]) -> None:
    body = _ok(fx_viewer_client, "/api/h2h", {"a": ids["hadley"], "b": ids["preston"]})
    assert (body["a"]["display_name"], body["b"]["display_name"]) == (
        "Hadley, Ike",
        "Abernathy, Preston",
    )
    assert (len(body["meetings"]), body["a_wins"], body["b_wins"], body["ties"]) == (
        249,
        53,
        177,
        19,
    )
    assert body["avg_margin"] == pytest.approx(-2.9759036, abs=1e-6)
    assert body["meetings"][0]["event_date"] == "2020-01-05"
    assert [m["event_date"] for m in body["meetings"]] == sorted(
        m["event_date"] for m in body["meetings"]
    )


def test_round_type_filter_restricts_rounds(
    fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]
) -> None:
    h2h = _ok(
        fx_viewer_client,
        "/api/h2h",
        {"a": ids["hadley"], "b": ids["preston"], "round_type": "super_sporting"},
    )
    assert [
        (m["event_date"], m["a_score"], m["b_score"], m["result"]) for m in h2h["meetings"]
    ] == [
        ("2026-09-06", 33, 40, "b"),
        ("2026-09-13", 34, 41, "b"),
    ]
    assert h2h["avg_margin"] == -7.0
    compare = _ok(
        fx_viewer_client,
        "/api/compare",
        {"ids": [ids["hadley"], ids["preston"], ids["noel"]], "round_type": "super_sporting"},
    )
    stats = [(s["n_rounds"], s["avg_score"], s["best_score"]) for s in compare["shooters"]]
    assert stats == [(2, 33.5, 34), (2, 40.5, 41), (2, 33.5, 36)]
    assert compare["shooters"][1]["podiums"] == 1
    assert [p["meetings"] for p in compare["pairs"]] == [2, 2, 2]
    # Every other fixture day has no station data, so it is `unknown`; none is plain `sporting`.
    unknown = _ok(
        fx_viewer_client, "/api/h2h", {"a": ids["hadley"], "b": ids["preston"], "round_type": "unknown"}
    )
    assert len(unknown["meetings"]) == 247
    both = _ok(
        fx_viewer_client,
        "/api/h2h",
        {"a": ids["hadley"], "b": ids["preston"], "round_type": ["sporting", "super_sporting"]},
    )
    assert len(both["meetings"]) == 2


def test_compare_three_shooters(
    fx_session: Session, fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]
) -> None:
    order = [ids["hadley"], ids["preston"], ids["noel"]]
    body = _ok(fx_viewer_client, "/api/compare", {"ids": order})
    shooters = body["shooters"]
    assert [s["shooter_id"] for s in shooters] == order
    table = [
        (s["n_rounds"], s["n_events"], s["median_score"], s["best_score"], s["wins"], s["podiums"])
        for s in shooters
    ]
    assert table == [
        (267, 267, 36.0, 45, 2, 14),
        (286, 285, 38.0, 47, 8, 51),
        (242, 240, 37.5, 45, 4, 28),
    ]
    assert [s["avg_score"] for s in shooters] == pytest.approx(
        [35.3071, 38.2483, 37.1033], abs=1e-4
    )
    pairs = [
        (p["a"], p["b"], p["meetings"], p["a_wins"], p["b_wins"], p["ties"]) for p in body["pairs"]
    ]
    assert pairs == [
        (ids["hadley"], ids["preston"], 249, 53, 177, 19),
        (ids["hadley"], ids["noel"], 209, 66, 132, 11),
        (ids["preston"], ids["noel"], 222, 111, 95, 16),
    ]
    for s in shooters:
        mus = (
            fx_session.execute(
                text("SELECT mu FROM rating_history WHERE shooter_id = :s ORDER BY event_date"),
                {"s": s["shooter_id"]},
            )
            .scalars()
            .all()
        )
        assert s["current_mu"] == pytest.approx(mus[-1])
        assert s["peak_mu"] == pytest.approx(max(mus))
    n_points = fx_session.execute(
        text("SELECT count(*) FROM rating_history WHERE shooter_id = ANY(:ids)"), {"ids": order}
    ).scalar_one()
    assert len(body["rating"]) == n_points


def test_compare_rejects_bad_id_counts(fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]) -> None:
    hadley = ids["hadley"]
    assert _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/compare", {"ids": [hadley]}, 400) == "invalid_compare"
    assert _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/compare", {"ids": [hadley, hadley]}, 400) == "invalid_compare"
    five = [hadley, ids["preston"], ids["noel"], hadley + 1, hadley + 2]
    assert _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/compare", {"ids": five}, 400) == "invalid_compare"
    assert _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/compare", None, 422) == "validation"


def test_unknown_shooter_is_404(fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]) -> None:
    missing = 999_999
    assert (
        _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/compare", {"ids": [ids["hadley"], missing]}, 404)
        == "shooter_not_found"
    )
    assert (
        _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/h2h", {"a": missing, "b": ids["hadley"]}, 404)
        == "shooter_not_found"
    )
    assert _error(fx_viewer_client, f"/api/shooters/{missing}/rivals", None, 404) == (
        "shooter_not_found"
    )


def test_h2h_same_shooter_is_400(fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]) -> None:
    params = {"a": ids["hadley"], "b": ids["hadley"]}
    assert _error(fx_viewer_client, "/api/h2h", params, 400) == "invalid_h2h"


def test_rivals_and_nemesis(
    fx_session: Session, fx_viewer_client: TestClient, ids: dict[str, int]
) -> None:
    hadley = ids["hadley"]
    body = _ok(fx_viewer_client, f"/api/shooters/{hadley}/rivals")
    assert body["shooter_id"] == hadley
    nemesis = body["nemesis"]
    assert nemesis["shooter_id"] == _shooter_id(fx_session, "fullerton tate")
    assert (nemesis["display_name"], nemesis["meetings"]) == ("Fullerton, Tate", 173)
    assert (nemesis["wins"], nemesis["losses"], nemesis["ties"]) == (10, 160, 3)
    rivals = body["rivals"]
    assert len(rivals) == 5
    scores = [r["rivalry"] for r in rivals]
    assert scores == sorted(scores, reverse=True)
    for rival in rivals:
        assert rival["meetings"] >= 5
        assert rival["rivalry"] == pytest.approx(rival["meetings"] / (1 + rival["mean_mu_gap"]))
        h2h = _ok(fx_viewer_client, "/api/h2h", {"a": hadley, "b": rival["shooter_id"]})
        assert (rival["meetings"], rival["wins"], rival["losses"], rival["ties"]) == (
            len(h2h["meetings"]),
            h2h["a_wins"],
            h2h["b_wins"],
            h2h["ties"],
        )
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the routes**

Create `backend/src/sunday_clays/api/routes/compare.py` (routers are auto-discovered; `ids` is a repeated query
parameter, which openapi-fetch sends as `ids=1&ids=2`):
```python
"""Compare 2-4 shooters, head-to-head records and automatic rivals."""

from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import date
from typing import Annotated, Final, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Path, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from sunday_clays.analytics import frames, rivals
from sunday_clays.api.routes._filters import round_type_param
from sunday_clays.db import get_session
from sunday_clays.domain.errors import DomainError, NotFoundError
from sunday_clays.domain.round_type import RoundType

router = APIRouter()
SessionDep = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_session, scope="function")]
MIN_COMPARE: Final = 2
MAX_COMPARE: Final = 4


class CompareShooterOut(BaseModel):
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str
    n_rounds: int
    n_events: int
    avg_score: float | None
    median_score: float | None
    best_score: int | None
    wins: int
    podiums: int
    current_mu: float | None
    peak_mu: float | None
    peak_date: date | None


class ComparePairOut(BaseModel):
    a: int
    b: int
    meetings: int
    a_wins: int
    b_wins: int
    ties: int


class CompareRatingPointOut(BaseModel):
    shooter_id: int
    event_date: date
    mu: float


class CompareOut(BaseModel):
    shooters: list[CompareShooterOut]
    pairs: list[ComparePairOut]
    rating: list[CompareRatingPointOut]


class H2HShooterOut(BaseModel):
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str


class H2HMeetingOut(BaseModel):
    event_date: date
    a_score: int
    b_score: int
    a_rank: int
    b_rank: int
    result: Literal["a", "b", "tie"]


class H2HOut(BaseModel):
    a: H2HShooterOut
    b: H2HShooterOut
    meetings: list[H2HMeetingOut]
    a_wins: int
    b_wins: int
    ties: int
    avg_margin: float | None


class RivalOut(BaseModel):
    shooter_id: int
    display_name: str
    meetings: int
    wins: int
    losses: int
    ties: int
    mean_mu_gap: float | None
    rivalry: float | None


class RivalsOut(BaseModel):
    shooter_id: int
    rivals: list[RivalOut]
    nemesis: RivalOut | None


def _require_shooters(names: Mapping[int, str], ids: Sequence[int]) -> None:
    for shooter_id in ids:
        if shooter_id not in names:
            raise NotFoundError("shooter_not_found", f"No shooter with id {shooter_id}.")


@router.get("/api/compare")
def compare(
    session: SessionDep,
    ids: Annotated[list[int], Query()],
    round_types: list[RoundType] = round_type_param,
) -> CompareOut:
    if len(set(ids)) != len(ids) or not MIN_COMPARE <= len(ids) <= MAX_COMPARE:
        raise DomainError("invalid_compare", "Pick 2 to 4 different shooters to compare.")
    names = rivals.display_names(session)
    _require_shooters(names, ids)
    rounds = frames.apply_round_type_filter(rivals.compare_rounds(session), round_types)
    history = rivals.rating_history(session)
    best = rivals.best_rounds(rounds)
    pairs = [rivals.head_to_head(best, a, b) for a, b in itertools.combinations(ids, 2)]
    return CompareOut(
        shooters=[
            CompareShooterOut.model_validate(asdict(s))
            for s in rivals.compare_stats(rounds, history, ids, names)
        ],
        pairs=[
            ComparePairOut(
                a=p.a,
                b=p.b,
                meetings=len(p.meetings),
                a_wins=p.a_wins,
                b_wins=p.b_wins,
                ties=p.ties,
            )
            for p in pairs
        ],
        rating=[
            CompareRatingPointOut.model_validate(asdict(p))
            for p in rivals.rating_points(history, ids)
        ],
    )


@router.get("/api/h2h")
def h2h(
    session: SessionDep,
    a: int,
    b: int,
    round_types: list[RoundType] = round_type_param,
) -> H2HOut:
    if a == b:
        raise DomainError("invalid_h2h", "Pick two different shooters.")
    names = rivals.display_names(session)
    _require_shooters(names, (a, b))
    rounds = frames.apply_round_type_filter(rivals.compare_rounds(session), round_types)
    result = rivals.head_to_head(rivals.best_rounds(rounds), a, b)
    return H2HOut(
        a=H2HShooterOut(shooter_id=a, display_name=names[a]),
        b=H2HShooterOut(shooter_id=b, display_name=names[b]),
        meetings=[H2HMeetingOut.model_validate(asdict(m)) for m in result.meetings],
        a_wins=result.a_wins,
        b_wins=result.b_wins,
        ties=result.ties,
        avg_margin=result.avg_margin,
    )


@router.get("/api/shooters/{id}/rivals")
def shooter_rivals(session: SessionDep, shooter_id: Annotated[int, Path(alias="id")]) -> RivalsOut:
    names = rivals.display_names(session)
    _require_shooters(names, (shooter_id,))
    best = rivals.best_rounds(rivals.compare_rounds(session))
    ranked, nemesis = rivals.rivals(best, shooter_id, names)
    return RivalsOut(
        shooter_id=shooter_id,
        rivals=[RivalOut.model_validate(asdict(r)) for r in ranked],
        nemesis=None if nemesis is None else RivalOut.model_validate(asdict(nemesis)),
    )
```

```ts
import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { expect, test } from './fixtures';

async function shooterId(page: Page, name: string): Promise<number> {
  const response = await page.request.get(`/api/shooters?q=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
  expect(response.ok()).toBe(true);
  const rows = (await response.json()) as { shooter_id: number; display_name: string }[];
  const row = rows.find((r) => r.display_name === name);
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`${name} is not in the seeded fixtures`);
  return row.shooter_id;
}

test('two shooters side by side with their head-to-head record', async ({ page }) => {
  const hadley = await shooterId(page, 'Hadley, Ike');
  const preston = await shooterId(page, 'Abernathy, Preston');
  await page.goto(`/compare?ids=${hadley},${preston}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Compare shooters' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Side by side' })).toBeVisible();
  const h2h = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Head to head' });
  await expect(h2h.getByText(/Hadley, Ike 53–177–19 Abernathy, Preston · 249 meetings/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(h2h.getByRole('button', { name: 'Table' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(h2h.getByRole('button', { name: 'CSV' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('the profile lists rivals and the nemesis', async ({ page }) => {
  const hadley = await shooterId(page, 'Hadley, Ike');
  await page.goto(`/shooters/${hadley}`);
  const rivals = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Rivals and nemesis' });
  await expect(rivals.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Nemesis' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(rivals.getByRole('link', { name: /Fullerton, Tate/ })).toBeVisible();
});
```

```ts
};

/**
 * `GET /api/shooters` belongs to the shooters feature (Plan 08), so it is not a default handler
 * here; tests that render the picker install this one with `server.use`.
 */
export const directoryHandler = http.get('*/api/shooters', () => HttpResponse.json(DIRECTORY));

export const handlers = [
  http.get('*/api/compare', () => HttpResponse.json(COMPARE_TWO)),
  http.get('*/api/h2h', () => HttpResponse.json(H2H)),
  http.get('*/api/shooters/:id/rivals', () => HttpResponse.json(RIVALS)),
];
```

```ts
import type { components, paths } from '../../api/schema';

type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } }
  ? R
  : never;

export type CompareResult = components['schemas']['CompareOut'];
export type CompareShooter = components['schemas']['CompareShooterOut'];
export type HeadToHead = components['schemas']['H2HOut'];
export type Rivals = components['schemas']['RivalsOut'];
export type Rival = components['schemas']['RivalOut'];
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/format.ts`:
```ts
/** Wins–losses–ties, e.g. "53–177–19". */
export function formatRecord(wins: number, losses: number, ties: number): string {
  return `${wins}–${losses}–${ties}`;
}

export function formatNumber(value: number | null, digits = 1): string {
  return value === null ? '—' : value.toFixed(digits);
}

/** Signed margin with a real minus sign: "+2.0", "−3.0", "0.0". */
export function formatMargin(value: number | null): string {
  if (value === null) return '—';
  const rounded = Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
  if (rounded === 0) return '0.0';
  return rounded > 0 ? `+${rounded.toFixed(1)}` : `−${Math.abs(rounded).toFixed(1)}`;
}

/** The compare page URL for a set of shooters. */
export function compareHref(ids: number[]): string {
  return `/compare?ids=${ids.join(',')}`;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/charts.ts`:
```ts
import type { EChartsOption } from 'echarts';
import { BarChart, LineChart } from 'echarts/charts';
import { GridComponent, LegendComponent, TooltipComponent } from 'echarts/components';
import { use as registerECharts } from 'echarts/core';
import type { TabularData } from '../../components/charts/types';
import type { CompareResult, HeadToHead } from './api';

registerECharts([BarChart, LineChart, GridComponent, LegendComponent, TooltipComponent]);

const round2 = (value: number): number => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;

/** One rating line (published mu, C7) per compared shooter over time. */
export function ratingChart(result: CompareResult): { data: TabularData; option: EChartsOption } {
  const rows = result.shooters.flatMap((s) =>
    result.rating
      .filter((p) => p.shooter_id === s.shooter_id)
      .map((p) => ({ event_date: p.event_date, shooter: s.display_name, rating: round2(p.mu) })),
  );
  return {
    data: {
      columns: [
        { key: 'event_date', label: 'Event', type: 'date' },
        { key: 'shooter', label: 'Shooter', type: 'string' },
        { key: 'rating', label: 'Rating', type: 'number' },
      ],
      rows,
    },
    option: {
      grid: { left: 8, right: 16, top: 40, bottom: 8, containLabel: true },
      legend: {},
      tooltip: { trigger: 'axis' },
      xAxis: { type: 'time' },
      yAxis: { type: 'value', name: 'Rating', scale: true },
      series: result.shooters.map((s) => ({
        type: 'line' as const,
        name: s.display_name,
        showSymbol: false,
        data: result.rating
          .filter((p) => p.shooter_id === s.shooter_id)
          .map((p) => [p.event_date, round2(p.mu)]),
      })),
    },
  };
}

/** One bar per shared Sunday: A's best round minus B's (positive = A won). */
export function marginChart(h2h: HeadToHead): { data: TabularData; option: EChartsOption } {
  const rows = h2h.meetings.map((m) => ({
    event_date: m.event_date,
    a_score: m.a_score,
    b_score: m.b_score,
    margin: m.a_score - m.b_score,
  }));
  return {
    data: {
      columns: [
        { key: 'event_date', label: 'Event', type: 'date' },
        { key: 'a_score', label: h2h.a.display_name, type: 'int' },
        { key: 'b_score', label: h2h.b.display_name, type: 'int' },
        { key: 'margin', label: 'Margin', type: 'int' },
      ],
      rows,
    },
    option: {
      grid: { left: 8, right: 16, top: 16, bottom: 8, containLabel: true },
      tooltip: { trigger: 'axis' },
      xAxis: { type: 'category', data: rows.map((r) => r.event_date) },
      yAxis: { type: 'value', name: `${h2h.a.display_name} minus ${h2h.b.display_name}` },
      series: [{ type: 'bar', name: 'Margin', data: rows.map((r) => r.margin) }],
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 20: Implement the query hooks, the picker and the rivals section**

Replace the whole content of `frontend/src/features/compare/api.ts` (adds the hooks; compare and head-to-head follow
the global round-type filter):
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../../api/client';
import type { components, paths } from '../../api/schema';
import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';

type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } }
  ? R
  : never;

export type CompareResult = components['schemas']['CompareOut'];
export type CompareShooter = components['schemas']['CompareShooterOut'];
export type HeadToHead = components['schemas']['H2HOut'];
export type Rivals = components['schemas']['RivalsOut'];
export type Rival = components['schemas']['RivalOut'];
/** One row of Plan 06's `GET /api/shooters` (only `shooter_id` and `display_name` are read). */
export type ShooterListing = JsonOf<paths['/api/shooters']['get']>[number];

/** Every shooter (Plan 06 directory), used for names and the picker's search. */
export function useShooterDirectory() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters'],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters')),
  });
}

export function useCompare(ids: number[]) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/compare', ids, roundTypes],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/compare', { params: { query: { ids, round_type: roundTypes } } })),
    enabled: ids.length >= 2,
  });
}

export function useHeadToHead(a: number, b: number) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/h2h', a, b, roundTypes],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/h2h', { params: { query: { a, b, round_type: roundTypes } } })),
  });
}

export function useRivals(shooterId: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}/rivals', shooterId],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}/rivals', { params: { path: { id: shooterId } } })),
  });
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/components/ShooterPicker.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, useState } from 'react';
import type { ShooterListing } from '../api';

export const MAX_COMPARED = 4;
const MAX_MATCHES = 8;

/** Search the directory by name and add up to 4 shooters; selected ones show as removable chips. */
export function ShooterPicker({
  directory,
  selected,
  onChange,
}: {
  directory: ShooterListing[];
  selected: number[];
  onChange: (ids: number[]) => void;
}) {
  const inputId = useId();
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const names = new Map(directory.map((s) => [s.shooter_id, s.display_name]));
  const needle = text.trim().toLowerCase();
  const matches =
    needle.length < 2
      ? []
      : directory
          .filter((s) => s.display_name.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
          .slice(0, MAX_MATCHES);
  const full = selected.length >= MAX_COMPARED;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <ul aria-label="Selected shooters" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {selected.map((id) => {
          const name = names.get(id) ?? `Shooter #${id}`;
          return (
            <li key={id}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onChange(selected.filter((s) => s !== id))}
                className="min-h-11 rounded-button border border-outline-variant px-3"
              >
                Remove {name}
              </button>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      <label htmlFor={inputId}>Find a shooter</label>
      <input
        id={inputId}
        type="search"
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="Type at least 2 letters"
        className="min-h-11 rounded-button bg-surface px-3"
      />
      {full ? <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">You can compare up to 4 shooters.</p> : null}
      <ul aria-label="Matching shooters" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        {matches.map((s) => (
          <li key={s.shooter_id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={full || selected.includes(s.shooter_id)}
              onClick={() => {
                onChange([...selected, s.shooter_id]);
                setText('');
              }}
              className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-button px-3 text-left disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Add {s.display_name}
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/components/RivalsSection.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { useRivals, type Rival } from '../api';
import { compareHref, formatRecord } from '../format';

function RivalLine({ shooterId, rival }: { shooterId: number; rival: Rival }) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={compareHref([shooterId, rival.shooter_id])}
      className="flex min-h-11 items-center justify-between gap-2 underline"
    >
      <span>{rival.display_name}</span>
      <span className="text-text-muted">
        {rival.meetings} Sundays · {formatRecord(rival.wins, rival.losses, rival.ties)}
      </span>
    </Link>
  );
}

/** Profile section: automatic rivals and the nemesis (C8 `/api/shooters/{id}/rivals`). */
export function RivalsSection({ shooterId }: { shooterId: number }) {
  const query = useRivals(shooterId);
  if (query.isPending) return <p role="status">Loading rivals…</p>;
  if (query.isError) return <p role="alert">Could not load rivals.</p>;
  const { rivals, nemesis } = query.data;
  return (
    <section aria-label="Rivals and nemesis" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {rivals.length === 0 ? (
        <p>No rivals yet. A rival needs at least 5 shared Sundays and a rating.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {rivals.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.shooter_id}>
              <RivalLine shooterId={shooterId} rival={r} />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {nemesis === null ? null : (
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-medium">Nemesis</h3>
          <RivalLine shooterId={shooterId} rival={nemesis} />
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
};

describe('ComparePage', () => {

      within(h2h).getByText('Hadley, Ike 0–2–0 Abernathy, Preston · 2 meetings · average margin −7.0'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(query.getAll('ids')).toEqual(['59', '194']);
    expect(query.getAll('round_type')).toEqual(['super_sporting']);
  });

    server.use(
      directoryHandler,

    // Kaplan shot no rounds of the filtered round type: scores are blank, ratings remain.

    );
    renderWithProviders(<ComparePage />, { route: '/compare?ids=59,999' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('No shooter with id 999.');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Find a shooter')).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it('shows the server message when the head-to-head fails', async () => {
    server.use(
      directoryHandler,
      http.get('*/api/compare', () => HttpResponse.json(COMPARE_TWO)),
      http.get('*/api/h2h', () =>
        HttpResponse.json(
          { error: { code: 'invalid_h2h', message: 'Pick two different shooters.' } },
          { status: 400 },
        ),
      ),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<ComparePage />, { route: '/compare?ids=59,194' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Pick two different shooters.');
  });

  it('reports a failed shooter list', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get('*/api/shooters', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'internal', message: 'x' } }, { status: 500 }),
      ),
    );
    renderWithProviders(<ComparePage />, { route: '/compare' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Could not load the shooter list.');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from 'react-router';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { server } from '../../test/msw/server';
import { directoryHandler } from './mocks';
import { profileSection } from './profileSection';
import { nav, routes } from './routes';

function renderAt(path: string) {
  // A parent route with a HydrateFallback, as in the app's layout route (and Plan 08's route
  // tests), so the lazy child loads without React Router's "no HydrateFallback" warning.
  const router = createMemoryRouter(
    [
      {
        path: '/',
        HydrateFallback: () => null,
        children: [
          ...routes,
          { path: '/shooters/:id', element: <profileSection.Component shooterId={59} /> },
        ],
      },
    ],
    { initialEntries: [path] },
  );
  render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient()}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('compare feature registration', () => {
  it('serves the lazy compare page on its default handlers', async () => {
    server.use(directoryHandler);
    renderAt('/compare?ids=59,194');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Compare shooters' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Head to head' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(nav).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ path: '/compare', order: 100 })]);
  });

  it('adds the rivals section to profiles', async () => {
    renderAt('/shooters/59');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: /Fullerton, Tate/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/components/CompareTable.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { CompareShooter } from '../api';
import { formatNumber } from '../format';

type Row = { label: string; value: (s: CompareShooter) => string };

const ROWS: Row[] = [
  { label: 'Rounds', value: (s) => String(s.n_rounds) },
  { label: 'Events', value: (s) => String(s.n_events) },
  { label: 'Average', value: (s) => formatNumber(s.avg_score, 2) },
  { label: 'Median', value: (s) => formatNumber(s.median_score) },
  { label: 'Best round', value: (s) => (s.best_score === null ? '—' : String(s.best_score)) },
  { label: 'Wins', value: (s) => String(s.wins) },
  { label: 'Podiums', value: (s) => String(s.podiums) },
  { label: 'Current rating', value: (s) => formatNumber(s.current_mu) },
  {
    label: 'Peak rating',
    value: (s) =>
      s.peak_mu === null || s.peak_date === null
        ? '—'
        : `${formatNumber(s.peak_mu)} (${s.peak_date})`,
  },
];

/** Side-by-side stats; scores follow the round-type filter, ratings never do (C7). */
export function CompareTable({ shooters }: { shooters: CompareShooter[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-card bg-elevated p-4">
      <table className="w-full text-left">
        <caption className="text-left text-lg font-medium">Side by side</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col">Stat</th>
            {shooters.map((s) => (
              <th key={s.shooter_id} scope="col">
                {s.display_name}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {ROWS.map((row) => (
            <tr key={row.label}>
              <th scope="row" className="text-text-muted">
                {row.label}
              </th>
              {shooters.map((s) => (
                <td key={s.shooter_id}>{row.value(s)}</td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/components/HeadToHeadCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import { useHeadToHead } from '../api';
import { marginChart } from '../charts';
import { formatMargin, formatRecord } from '../format';

/** Two shooters' best rounds on every Sunday they both shot (ties count as ties). */
export function HeadToHeadCard({ a, b }: { a: number; b: number }) {
  const query = useHeadToHead(a, b);
  if (query.isPending) return <p role="status">Loading head-to-head…</p>;
  if (query.isError) return <p role="alert">{query.error.message}</p>;
  const h2h = query.data;
  const chart = marginChart(h2h);
  return (
    <ChartFrame
      title="Head to head"
      subtitle={`${h2h.a.display_name} ${formatRecord(h2h.a_wins, h2h.b_wins, h2h.ties)} ${h2h.b.display_name} · ${h2h.meetings.length} meetings · average margin ${formatMargin(h2h.avg_margin)}`}
      option={chart.option}
      columns={chart.data.columns}
      rows={chart.data.rows}
      csvName="head-to-head"
      ariaLabel={`Bar chart of ${h2h.a.display_name}'s margin over ${h2h.b.display_name} on each shared Sunday`}
      urlKey="h2h"
    />
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/compare/pages/ComparePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ChartFrame } from '../../../components/charts/ChartFrame';
import { intCodec, listCodec, useUrlState } from '../../../lib/useUrlState';
import { useCompare, useShooterDirectory, type CompareResult } from '../api';
import { ratingChart } from '../charts';
import { CompareTable } from '../components/CompareTable';
import { HeadToHeadCard } from '../components/HeadToHeadCard';
import { ShooterPicker } from '../components/ShooterPicker';
import { formatRecord } from '../format';

const idsCodec = listCodec(intCodec);
const NO_IDS: number[] = [];

function Pairs({ result }: { result: CompareResult }) {
  const names = new Map(result.shooters.map((s) => [s.shooter_id, s.display_name]));
  return (
    <section aria-label="Pairwise records" className="rounded-card bg-elevated p-4">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">Pairwise records</h2>
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        {result.pairs.map((p) => (
          <li key={`${p.a}-${p.b}`}>
            {names.get(p.a)} {formatRecord(p.a_wins, p.b_wins, p.ties)} {names.get(p.b)} in{' '}
            {p.meetings} meetings
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

function Comparison({ ids }: { ids: number[] }) {
  const query = useCompare(ids);
  if (query.isPending) return <p role="status">Loading comparison…</p>;
  if (query.isError) return <p role="alert">{query.error.message}</p>;
  const chart = ratingChart(query.data);
  const [a, b] = ids;
  return (
    <>
      <CompareTable shooters={query.data.shooters} />
      <ChartFrame
        title="Rating over time"
        subtitle="Ratings ignore the round-type filter"
        option={chart.option}
        columns={chart.data.columns}
        rows={chart.data.rows}
        csvName="compare-ratings"
        ariaLabel="Line chart of each compared shooter's rating over time"
        urlKey="cr"
      />
      {ids.length === 2 && a !== undefined && b !== undefined ? (
        <HeadToHeadCard a={a} b={b} />
      ) : (
        <Pairs result={query.data} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export function ComparePage() {
  const [ids, setIds] = useUrlState('ids', idsCodec, NO_IDS);
  const directory = useShooterDirectory();
  return (
    <main className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Compare shooters</h1>
      {directory.isError ? (
        <p role="alert">Could not load the shooter list.</p>
      ) : (
        <ShooterPicker directory={directory.data ?? []} selected={ids} onChange={setIds} />
      )}
      {ids.length < 2 ? (
        <p className="text-text-muted">Pick at least two shooters to compare.</p>
      ) : (
        <Comparison ids={ids} />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
```

### Task 4: Year in Review and On this day (master Plan 11 T4)

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/sunday_clays/analytics/yir.py`
- Create: `backend/src/sunday_clays/api/routes/yir.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/unit/extras/test_extras_yir.py`, `backend/tests/integration/extras/test_extras_yir_routes.py`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/yir/api.ts`, `format.ts`, `charts.ts`, `mocks.ts`, `routes.tsx`, `homeWidget.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/yir/components/YirCard.tsx`, `components/Stat.tsx`, `components/LeaderList.tsx`, `components/OnThisDayCard.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/yir/pages/YirIndexPage.tsx`, `pages/YirClubPage.tsx`, `pages/YirShooterPage.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/src/features/yir/format.test.ts`, `charts.test.ts`, `routes.test.tsx`, `components/OnThisDayCard.test.tsx`, `pages/YirIndexPage.test.tsx`, `pages/YirClubPage.test.tsx`, `pages/YirShooterPage.test.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/e2e/yir.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 06 / C7): `sunday_clays.analytics.frames.load_rounds(session)` (C7 columns incl. `event_date,
  shooter_id, display_name, score, is_best_round, event_rank`), `load_rating_history(session)` (`shooter_id,
  event_date, mu, var`); `cache.cached_by_data_version`; `domain.errors.NotFoundError(code, message)` (404, rendered
  as `{"error": {"code", "message"}}`); `api.routes._filters.resolve_as_of(as_of: date | None, tz: str) -> date` and
  `today_local(tz: str) -> date` (Plan 06 T1: routes resolve "today" only through this module; tests monkeypatch
  `_filters.today_local`); `config.get_settings()` (`timezone`); `db.get_session`.
- Consumes (C4 tables, read with `text()` SQL): `events(event_date, has_scores, results_complete, head_count,
  n_shooters)`, `event_metrics(event_date, median, top_score, difficulty)`, `shooter_profiles(shooter_id,
  display_name, left_censored)`, `achievements_awarded(shooter_id, code, event_date)` (Plan 10 T1 writes it),
  `rating_history`, `shooter_aliases(name_key, shooter_id)` (tests). C12 `personal_bests`: a day is a PB day when the
  day's best round beats every earlier round and the shooter has ≥5 earlier rounds.
- Consumes (Plan 09 HTTP, Decision 4): `GET /api/leaderboards?period=season&metric=<events|avg_score|wins|season_points>
  [&as_of=YYYY-MM-DD][&round_type=…]` → `LeaderboardOut { period, metric, as_of, min_rounds_applied, n_eligible,
  event_dates, rows: LeaderboardRowOut { rank, shooter_id, display_name, status, value, n_rounds }[] }` (Plan 09 T1/T2;
  Plan 09 T5a's `useLeaderboard` keys it `['/api/leaderboards', query]` and omits an empty `round_type`).
- Consumes (Plan 07, Decision 3): `api`, `unwrap`; `components`, `paths`; `getMe()` (`src/lib/me.ts`, the "That's
  me" id or `null`; tests use `setMe`/`clearMe`); `useRoundTypes(): [RoundTypeValue[], setter]` (`src/lib/roundTypes.ts`,
  the global `rt` filter; `useSeasonBoard` sends it as `round_type`, C10); `NavItem`; `ChartFrame`; `TabularData`;
  `renderWithProviders(ui, { route?, path? })` (returns `{ user, router, … }`); `server`; e2e `test`/`expect`.
- Consumes (Plan 08 T4): `HomeWidget = { id; order; slot: 'main' | 'me'; Component: FC<{ meId: number | null }> }`
  (`src/features/home/widgets.ts`).
- Produces (HTTP): `GET /api/yir/{year}` (2000..2100, else 422) → `YirClubOut { year; years: int[]; totals:
  YirTotalsOut; events; newcomers; perfect_rounds; top_rounds: YirTopRoundOut[]; mean_head_count; busiest | hardest |
  easiest: YirEventStatOut | null; trophies; months: YirMonthOut[12]; previous: YirTotalsOut | null }` with
  `YirTotalsOut { year, scored_events, held_events, rounds, shooters, clays_thrown, clays_broken, avg_score }`,
  `YirTopRoundOut { shooter_id, display_name, event_date, score }`, `YirEventStatOut { event_date, value }`,
  `YirMonthOut { month, events, rounds, avg_score }`; `GET /api/yir/{year}/shooters/{id}` → `YirShooterOut { year,
  shooter_id, display_name, totals: YirShooterTotalsOut, best: YirTopRoundOut | null, wins, podiums, best_finish,
  pbs: { event_date, score }[], trophies, rating_start, rating_end, attendance_rank, n_shooters, months: { month,
  rounds, avg_score, club_avg_score }[12], previous: YirShooterTotalsOut | null }` (404 `shooter_not_found`);
  `GET /api/on-this-day[?date=YYYY-MM-DD]` → `OnThisDayOut { on; items: { years_ago, event_date, has_scores,
  head_count, n_shooters, top_score, median, winners: { shooter_id, display_name, score }[] }[] }`. Frontend: routes
  `/yir` (redirects to the latest scored year), `/yir/:year`, `/yir/:year/shooters/:id`; nav `{ label: 'Year in
  Review', path: '/yir', icon: CalendarRange, order: 110 }`; `homeWidget` (`id: 'on-this-day'`, `order: 40`, `slot:
  'main'`); `YirCard({ title, children })` (a region named `title`; Task 5 adds its share button); MSW default
  handlers for `/api/yir/:year`, `/api/yir/:year/shooters/:id`, `/api/on-this-day`, and the non-default
  `leaderboardHandler` for `/api/leaderboards`.
