### Task 5: Share images for events, profiles, trophies and Year in Review cards (master Plan 11 T5)

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/features/share/filenames.ts`, `ShareCard.tsx`, `api.ts`, `format.ts`, `fixtures.ts`, `eventSection.tsx`, `profileSection.tsx`
- Create: `frontend/src/features/share/components/EventShareCard.tsx`, `components/ProfileShareCard.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/achievements/components/TrophyCard.tsx` (Plan 10 T7's card: wrapped in `ShareCard`,
  props and link unchanged)
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/yir/components/YirCard.tsx` (Task 4's card: wrapped in `ShareCard`)
- Test: `frontend/src/features/share/filenames.test.ts`, `ShareCard.test.tsx`, `sections.test.tsx`, `components/EventShareCard.test.tsx`, `components/ProfileShareCard.test.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/src/features/achievements/components/TrophyCard.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/features/yir/components/YirCard.test.tsx`
- Test: `frontend/e2e/share.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 07 T4, Decision 3): `shareElementAsImage(el: HTMLElement, filename: string):
  Promise<ShareOutcome>` from `src/lib/share.ts` (`ShareOutcome = 'shared' | 'downloaded' | 'cancelled'`; renders the
  element with html-to-image, then uses the Web Share API when `navigator.canShare({ files })` is true, else downloads
  the PNG; resolves `'cancelled'` when the share sheet is dismissed, rejects when rendering fails). This task reads
  the result as `unknown` and only special-cases `'cancelled'`. Also `api`, `unwrap`; `paths`; `useRoundTypes()`
  (`src/lib/roundTypes.ts`; `GET /api/shooters/{id}` accepts `round_type`, so the share hook sends it, C10);
  `renderWithProviders(ui, { route?, path? })` (returns `{ user, router, … }`); `server`; e2e `test`/`expect`. Plan 01's
  `vitest.config.ts` has `restoreMocks: true` only, which never clears a `vi.fn()`'s calls, so each test file that
  mocks `lib/share` calls `vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockReset()` in `beforeEach`.
- Consumes (Plan 06 HTTP, Decision 2): `GET /api/events/{date}` → `EventDetailOut` (fields read: `event_date`,
  `round_type`, `has_scores`, `head_count`, `n_shooters`, `median`, `results[].{round_id, display_name, score,
  is_best_round, event_rank}`); `GET /api/shooters/{id}` → `ShooterDetailOut` (fields read: `display_name`,
  `deceased`, `odometer.{clays_broken, rounds, events, years_active, longest_streak}`, `pbs[].{scope, score,
  event_date}`; `PbOut.key` is `"all"` for the overall PB). Path keys are the C8 literals `/api/events/{date}` and
  `/api/shooters/{id}` (Plan 06 binds them with `Path(alias=…)`). Plan 08's `useEvent(date)` keys the event query
  `['/api/events/{date}', date]` and `useShooter(id)` keys the shooter query
  `['/api/shooters/{id}', id, { round_type: roundTypes }]`; this task uses the same keys, so the page and its Share
  section share one request.
- Consumes (Plan 08 T1/T2a glob hosts): `EventSection = { id; title; order; Component: FC<{ date: string }> }`
  (`src/features/events/sections.ts`) and `ProfileSection = { id; title; order; Component: FC<{ shooterId: number }>
  }` (`src/features/shooters/sections.ts`); each section is wrapped in a card titled `title`.
- Consumes (Plan 10 T7, Decision 5): `TrophyCard({ trophy }: { trophy: TrophyOut })` in
  `src/features/achievements/components/TrophyCard.tsx`, `TrophyIcon` (`./TrophyIcon`, props `artKey, metal, locked,
  size`), `formatPct` (`../labels`); `TrophyOut { code, family, name, description, category, art_key, metal, level,
  threshold, label, repeatable, holders, rarity_pct, last_awarded }`.
- Consumes (Plan 11 Task 4): `YirCard({ title, children })` in `src/features/yir/components/YirCard.tsx` (a region
  named `title`).
- Produces: `ShareCard({ filename, name?, children })` — renders `children` plus a "Share image" button (accessible
  name set with `aria-label`: `Share image: <name>` when `name` is given, else `Share image`) and a status line; the
  PNG is exactly the wrapped card.
  `filenames.ts`: `slugify(text)`, `eventFilename(date) = 'sunday-clays-<date>.png'`, `profileFilename(name) =
  'sunday-clays-<slug>.png'`, `trophyFilename(code) = 'sunday-clays-trophy-<slug>.png'`, `cardFilename(title) =
  'sunday-clays-<slug>.png'`. Glob exports `eventSection` and `profileSection` (both `id: 'share'`, `title: 'Share'`,
  `order: 95`).

**Branch:** `task/11-5-share-images`

**Depends on:** Plan 11 T1, T4 (this task runs alone in the last sub-wave, after Plan 10 T7 and Plan 11 T4); Plan 10
T7 (`TrophyCard.tsx`); Plan 08 T1 (event section glob), T2a (profile section glob); Plan 07 T4 (`lib/share.ts`,
client); Plan 06 T5/T6 (`/api/events/{date}`, `/api/shooters/{id}`); Plan 04 T2 (e2e fixture seed).

**Working rules for this task:**
- Frontend only. Vitest + Testing Library + MSW. `lib/share.ts` (html-to-image and the Web Share API) is the external
  boundary: unit tests replace it with `vi.mock(...)` and never render a real image. The event and shooter
  endpoints belong to Plan 06/08, so `share/fixtures.ts` exports test data only (no default MSW handlers); tests
  answer those requests with `server.use(...)`.
- This task edits two files it does not own: Plan 10's `TrophyCard.tsx` (its props, link and markup inside the card
  stay as they are) and Task 4's `YirCard.tsx`. Do not touch any other file under `features/achievements`,
  `features/events`, `features/shooters` or `features/yir`.
- Fixture facts for e2e (pandas over the fixture workbooks): 2026-09-13's top results are Nordquist, Sherman 42, Abernathy,
  Preston 41, Kolmanov 39, then a tie at 37 (rank 4); Hadley, Ike has 267 rounds, 9,427 clays broken and a best of 45.
- e2e: headless Chromium may expose the Web Share API, so `share.spec.ts` removes `navigator.share` and
  `navigator.canShare` with `page.addInitScript`, which makes `lib/share.ts` download the PNG; Playwright's
  `download.suggestedFilename()` is then the file name.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing e2e spec**

Create `frontend/e2e/share.spec.ts`:
```ts
import type { Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { expect, test } from './fixtures';

// Headless Chromium may expose the Web Share API; remove it so lib/share.ts always downloads.
test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'share', { value: undefined, configurable: true });
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'canShare', { value: undefined, configurable: true });
  });
});

async function shooterId(page: Page, name: string): Promise<number> {
  const response = await page.request.get(`/api/shooters?q=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
  expect(response.ok()).toBe(true);
  const rows = (await response.json()) as { shooter_id: number; display_name: string }[];
  const row = rows.find((r) => r.display_name === name);
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`${name} is not in the seeded fixtures`);
  return row.shooter_id;
}

async function download(page: Page, button: Locator): Promise<string> {
  await expect(button).toBeEnabled();
  const pending = page.waitForEvent('download');
  await button.click();
  return (await pending).suggestedFilename();
}

test('the event results card downloads as an image', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/events/2026-09-13');
  const section = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Shareable results card' });
  await expect(section.getByText('Nordquist, Sherman')).toBeVisible();
  const name = await download(page, section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image' }));
  expect(name).toBe('sunday-clays-2026-09-13.png');
});

test('the profile card downloads as an image', async ({ page }) => {
  const hadley = await shooterId(page, 'Hadley, Ike');
  await page.goto(`/shooters/${hadley}`);
  const section = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Shareable profile card' });
  const name = await download(page, section.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image' }));
  expect(name).toBe('sunday-clays-hadley-ike.png');
});

test('a year in review card downloads as an image', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/yir/2025');
  const button = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image: 2025 at a glance' });
  expect(await download(page, button)).toBe('sunday-clays-2025-at-a-glance.png');
});

test('a trophy card downloads as an image', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/achievements');
  const button = page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Share image: / }).first();
  expect(await download(page, button)).toMatch(/^sunday-clays-trophy-.+\.png$/);
});
```

Create `frontend/src/features/share/ShareCard.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { shareElementAsImage } from '../../lib/share';
import { ShareCard } from './ShareCard';

// html-to-image and the Web Share API are the external boundary; lib/share.ts owns them.
vi.mock('../../lib/share', () => ({ shareElementAsImage: vi.fn() }));

// restoreMocks (Plan 01) keeps a vi.fn()'s calls and implementation between tests.
beforeEach(() => {
  vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockReset();
});

function renderCard() {
  render(
    <ShareCard filename="sunday-clays-2026-09-13.png" name="Sep 13">
      <p>Results card</p>
    </ShareCard>,
  );
  return screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image: Sep 13' });
}

describe('ShareCard', () => {
  it('renders exactly the wrapped card under the given filename', async () => {
    vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockResolvedValue('downloaded');
    await userEvent.setup().click(renderCard());
    expect(shareElementAsImage).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const [element, filename] = vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(element).toHaveTextContent('Results card');
    expect(element).not.toHaveTextContent('Share image');
    expect(filename).toBe('sunday-clays-2026-09-13.png');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Image ready.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('stays quiet when the share sheet is dismissed', async () => {
    vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockResolvedValue('cancelled');
    await userEvent.setup().click(renderCard());
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Share image/ })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it('disables the button while the image is being made', async () => {
    let finish: (value: 'downloaded') => void = () => undefined;
    vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockReturnValue(new Promise((resolve) => (finish = resolve)));
    const button = renderCard();
    await userEvent.setup().click(button);
    expect(button).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('Preparing image…');
    finish('downloaded');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Image ready.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when the image could not be made', async () => {
    vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockRejectedValue(new Error('canvas tainted'));
    await userEvent.setup().click(renderCard());
    expect(await screen.findByText('Could not create the image.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('names the button plainly without a card name', () => {
    render(
      <ShareCard filename="x.png">
        <p>Card</p>
      </ShareCard>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

Create `frontend/src/features/share/filenames.ts`:
```ts
/** Lower-case ASCII words joined by dashes ("Hadley, Ike" -> "hadley-ike"); never empty. */
export function slugify(text: string): string {
  const slug = text
    .normalize('NFKD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') // combining marks left by NFKD (written as escapes)
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
  return slug === '' ? 'image' : slug;
}

export function eventFilename(date: string): string {
  return `sunday-clays-${date}.png`;
}

export function profileFilename(name: string): string {
  return `sunday-clays-${slugify(name)}.png`;
}

export function trophyFilename(code: string): string {
  return `sunday-clays-trophy-${slugify(code)}.png`;
}

export function cardFilename(title: string): string {
  return `sunday-clays-${slugify(title)}.png`;
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/share/ShareCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { shareElementAsImage } from '../../lib/share';

type Status = 'idle' | 'busy' | 'done' | 'failed';

const MESSAGES: Record<Status, string> = {
  idle: '',
  busy: 'Preparing image…',
  done: 'Image ready.',
  failed: 'Could not create the image.',
};

/**
 * Wraps a card and adds a "Share image" button that renders exactly the wrapped card to a PNG
 * through `lib/share.ts` (Web Share API with the file when the browser can, else a download).
 */
export function ShareCard({
  filename,
  name,
  children,
}: {
  filename: string;
  /** Appended to the button's accessible name when several cards share a page. */
  name?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const cardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>('idle');

  async function share() {
    setStatus('busy');
    try {
      const outcome: unknown = await shareElementAsImage(
        cardRef.current as HTMLDivElement,
        filename,
      );
      setStatus(outcome === 'cancelled' ? 'idle' : 'done');
    } catch {
      setStatus('failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div ref={cardRef}>{children}</div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {/* The name is set with aria-label, not a `sr-only` span: sr-only is position:absolute,
            so Chromium's name computation pads it with spaces ("Share image : x") and
            Playwright's `name: 'Share image: x'` would miss it. The label starts with the
            visible text (WCAG 2.5.3). */}
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={name === undefined ? 'Share image' : `Share image: ${name}`}
          onClick={() => void share()}
          disabled={status === 'busy'}
          className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-button border border-outline-variant px-4"
        >
          <Share2 aria-hidden="true" className="size-4" />
          Share image
        </button>
        <span role="status" className="text-sm text-text-muted">
          {MESSAGES[status]}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/share/format.ts`:
```ts
const INT = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US');
const FULL_DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  weekday: 'short',
  month: 'short',
  day: 'numeric',
  year: 'numeric',
  timeZone: 'UTC',
});

export const ROUND_TYPE_LABELS: Record<'sporting' | 'super_sporting' | 'unknown', string> = {
  sporting: 'Sporting',
  super_sporting: 'Super Sporting',
  unknown: 'Round type not recorded',
};

export function formatInt(value: number): string {
  return INT.format(value);
}

/** "Sun, Sep 13, 2026". */
export function formatFullDay(iso: string): string {
  return FULL_DAY.format(new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`));
}
```

Create `frontend/src/features/share/components/EventShareCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { EventDetail } from '../api';
import { ROUND_TYPE_LABELS, formatFullDay } from '../format';

export const TOP_RESULTS = 5;

/** A compact, image-friendly results card for one Sunday: the top 5 best rounds by rank. */
export function EventShareCard({ event }: { event: EventDetail }) {
  const top = event.results
    .flatMap((r) =>
      r.is_best_round && r.event_rank !== null ? [{ ...r, rank: r.event_rank }] : [],
    )
    .sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank || a.display_name.localeCompare(b.display_name))
    .slice(0, TOP_RESULTS);
  return (
    <article className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-card bg-surface p-4 text-text">
      <p className="text-sm text-accent">Sunday Clays</p>
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">{formatFullDay(event.event_date)}</h3>
      {event.has_scores ? (
        <>
          <p className="text-text-muted">
            {ROUND_TYPE_LABELS[event.round_type]} · {event.n_shooters} shooters
            {event.median === null ? '' : ` · median ${event.median}`}
          </p>
          <ol className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            {top.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.round_id} className="flex justify-between gap-4">
                <span>
                  {r.rank}. {r.display_name}
                </span>
                <span className="font-medium">{r.score}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </>
      ) : (
        <p>
          {event.head_count === null
            ? 'No scores recorded.'
            : `Attendance only: ${event.head_count} shooters, no scores recorded.`}
        </p>
      )}
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ShooterDetail } from '../api';
import { formatFullDay, formatInt } from '../format';

/** A lifetime odometer + personal best card for one shooter (C12 odometer fields). */
export function ProfileShareCard({ shooter }: { shooter: ShooterDetail }) {
  const pb = shooter.pbs.find((p) => p.scope === 'overall');
  const odometer = shooter.odometer;
  return (
    <article className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-card bg-surface p-4 text-text">
      <p className="text-sm text-accent">Sunday Clays</p>
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">{shooter.display_name}</h3>
      {shooter.deceased ? <p className="text-text-muted">In memory</p> : null}
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <dt className="text-text-muted">Personal best</dt>
        <dd>{pb === undefined ? '—' : `${pb.score} (${formatFullDay(pb.event_date)})`}</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-muted">Clays broken</dt>
        <dd>{formatInt(odometer.clays_broken)}</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-muted">Rounds</dt>
        <dd>{formatInt(odometer.rounds)}</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-muted">Sundays</dt>
        <dd>{formatInt(odometer.events)}</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-muted">Years active</dt>
        <dd>{odometer.years_active}</dd>
        <dt className="text-text-muted">Longest streak</dt>
        <dd>{odometer.longest_streak} Sundays</dd>
      </dl>
    </article>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Implement the query hooks and the two glob sections**

Replace the whole content of `frontend/src/features/share/api.ts` (adds `useEventDetail` and `useShooterDetail`; both
use Plan 08's query keys, and the shooter hook sends the global round-type filter like Plan 08's `useShooter`, C10):
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../../api/client';
import type { paths } from '../../api/schema';
import { useRoundTypes } from '../../lib/roundTypes';

type JsonOf<Op> = Op extends { responses: { 200: { content: { 'application/json': infer R } } } }
  ? R
  : never;

/** Plan 06's event detail; fields read: event_date, round_type, has_scores, head_count,
 * n_shooters, median, results[].{round_id, display_name, score, is_best_round, event_rank}. */
export type EventDetail = JsonOf<paths['/api/events/{date}']['get']>;
/** Plan 06's shooter detail; fields read: display_name, deceased, odometer, pbs. */
export type ShooterDetail = JsonOf<paths['/api/shooters/{id}']['get']>;

export function useEventDetail(date: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/events/{date}', date],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/events/{date}', { params: { path: { date } } })),
  });
}

export function useShooterDetail(shooterId: number) {
  const [roundTypes] = useRoundTypes();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['/api/shooters/{id}', shooterId, { round_type: roundTypes }],
    queryFn: () =>
      unwrap(
        api.GET('/api/shooters/{id}', {
          params: { path: { id: shooterId }, query: { round_type: roundTypes } },
        }),
      ),
  });
}
```

```tsx
      ) : (
        <ShareCard filename={eventFilename(date)}>

        </ShareCard>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

  title: 'Share',
  order: 95,

};
```

```tsx
  });

  it('names one-off trophies by their name alone', () => {
    renderWithProviders(
      <TrophyCard
        trophy={{
          ...TROPHY,
          code: 'first_win',
          name: 'First Win',
          label: null,
          metal: null,
          level: null,
          threshold: null,
        }}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image: First Win' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { shareElementAsImage } from '../../../lib/share';
import { YirCard } from './YirCard';

vi.mock('../../../lib/share', () => ({ shareElementAsImage: vi.fn() }));

// restoreMocks (Plan 01) keeps a vi.fn()'s call history between tests.
beforeEach(() => {
  vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockReset();
});

describe('YirCard sharing (Plan 11 Task 5)', () => {
  it('shares the card as sunday-clays-<title>.png', async () => {
    vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mockResolvedValue('downloaded');
    render(
      <YirCard title="2025 at a glance">
        <p>1,267 rounds</p>
      </YirCard>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: '2025 at a glance' })).toHaveTextContent(
      '1,267 rounds',
    );
    await userEvent
      .setup()
      .click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Share image: 2025 at a glance' }));
    const [element, filename] = vi.mocked(shareElementAsImage).mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(element).toHaveTextContent('1,267 rounds');
    expect(filename).toBe('sunday-clays-2025-at-a-glance.png');
  });
});
```

After this step it is:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import type { components } from '../../../api/schema';
import { ShareCard } from '../../share/ShareCard';
import { trophyFilename } from '../../share/filenames';
import { formatPct } from '../labels';
import { TrophyIcon } from './TrophyIcon';

type TrophyOut = components['schemas']['TrophyOut'];

export function TrophyCard({ trophy }: { trophy: TrophyOut }) {
  return (
    <ShareCard
      filename={trophyFilename(trophy.code)}
      name={trophy.label === null ? trophy.name : `${trophy.name}, ${trophy.label}`}
    >
      <Link
        to={`/achievements/${encodeURIComponent(trophy.code)}`}
        className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-elevated p-3"
      >
        <TrophyIcon
          artKey={trophy.art_key}
          metal={trophy.metal}
          locked={trophy.holders === 0}
          size={56}
        />
        <span className="flex flex-col">
          <span className="font-medium">{trophy.name}</span>
          {trophy.label === null ? null : (
            <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">{trophy.label}</span>
          )}
          <span className="text-xs text-text-muted">
            {trophy.holders} holders · {formatPct(trophy.rarity_pct)}
          </span>
        </span>
      </Link>
    </ShareCard>
  );
}
```

Replace the whole content of `frontend/src/features/yir/components/YirCard.tsx` (Task 4's card, now wrapped):
```tsx
import { useId, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ShareCard } from '../../share/ShareCard';
import { cardFilename } from '../../share/filenames';

/** A titled Year in Review card with a "Share image" button (Plan 11 Task 5). */
export function YirCard({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const titleId = useId();
  return (
    <ShareCard filename={cardFilename(title)} name={title}>
      <section
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-card bg-elevated p-4"
      >
        <h2 id={titleId} className="text-lg font-medium">
          {title}
        </h2>
        {children}
      </section>
    </ShareCard>
  );
}
```
